## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S0, amendment A1 (2026-10-20)

The validation of stage S0 at `41564767` passed every check except the source scan of checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md), which printed five hits, all in the new modules: the `=ᵈ` notation of `VCVioForwardEvalDist` and the `vcvio_legacy` tactic macro, the two command syntaxes and the `addDecl` of the twin command in `VCVioLegacyBridge`. Amendment A1 of the plan (Claude main thread, 2026-10-19, recorded in the [plan copy](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md#amendments)) keeps that scan a zero-hit gate rather than allowlisting the hits, and the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19 limits the migration's validation to Lean-level checks. Claude applied A1 on the same branch, `codex/vcvio-migration-S0`, without rewriting pushed commits: `765e060a` (Lean modules, inventory, trust-boundary section and plan copy) and the commit that adds this entry, the updated section [VCVio deprecation migration, stage S0: forward vocabulary and the transitional bridge (2026-10-19)](../formal-verification-analysis.md#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s0-forward-vocabulary-and-the-transitional-bridge-2026-10-19) and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s0-a1.json`. The receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s0.json` stays the record of `41564767`. No statement changed.

What changed. `VCVioForwardEvalDist` no longer declares `infix:50 " =ᵈ " => EvalDistEq`: its 16 theorem statements and the 6 of `VCVioForwardOracleComp` that used the notation write `EvalDistEq A B`, which elaborates to the same term, and the docstrings name upstream's notation. `VCVioLegacyBridge` keeps only its 14 guarded bridge lemmas; the tactic, the certificate and twin commands, the pre-stage dump reader and the inverse vocabulary map moved to the scratch module `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/tools/CertTools.lean`, which imports only Lean and `VCVio.EvalDist.ProbabilityNotation`, is compiled separately and is imported only by out-of-build certificate files. Transitional twins are now generated source text: the tool prints, for a declaration `T`, the command `set_option linter.deprecated false in theorem _root_.T_vcvioLegacy : <old statement> := by simpa only [<bridge lemmas>] using @T` (for a wrapper `W`, the equation `@W = <old body>` proved by `delta W; simp only [...]`), with the position after `T` in `T`'s module; when the printed statement names binders that its text never references (for example instance binders such as `inst`), the header also carries `set_option linter.unusedVariables false in`. In `check-inventory.sh`, the pin of the notation line became a pin of the definition text of `EvalDistEq`, and the three pins of the tactic and command declarations became, for each of the five migration modules, zero-count pins for notation, syntax, elaborator, initializer and simp-attribute declarations and for `import Lean`, `addDecl`, `Lean.Elab`, `Lean.Meta`, `run_cmd` and `#eval`.

Tool corrections found by the self-test, before any twin entered the build. The twin printer elaborated each candidate twin in the certificate file's environment and accepted the first that produced no message, but Lean checked the twin's proof in a snapshot task whose messages the printer did not read, so the first candidate was always accepted; one of ten self-test twins (`VCVioFeasibility.ctx_binding_bound_tight`, an oracle computation over `CMOracle`) then failed to compile, because the `ProbComp` bridge does not apply to it. The candidate is now elaborated synchronously (probe: a `False := by exact trivial` command reported no message before and its error after the change). The round-trip check compared the re-elaborated statement with the rebuilt one only up to binder names, so two twins (`StatefulGoodEvent.output_good` and `event_good`) printed named hypotheses such as `agree_good` as plain arrows and had the dump's type hash but not its `pp.all` text; the printer now keeps the names of named hypotheses and requires the `pp.all` text. The certificate proofs now run without error recovery and reject a proof term with `sorry` or metavariables, round-trip attempts no longer leak deprecation warnings into the output, and the twin insertion script reads Lean 4.34's unprefixed info output.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S0/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell on the A1 Lean sources, 2026-10-19 23:18 to 23:40 (lease 00000763): 5,535 jobs, 0 errors, so every axiom guard holds; on the clean committed tree `765e060a` (lease 00000765) it rebuilt nothing and completed the 5,535 jobs with rc 0.
- Gates: G1, deprecation warnings of the deprecated VCVio declarations, 1,359 to 1,338 as before (the 21 warned sites of S0), no increase in any file, no new warning category; G2, 1,724 deprecated source sites outside the bridge and 182 suppression lines; G3, 756 deprecated-interface statements and bodies before and after; G4, the declaration dump has 95,198 declarations (95,092 at the base): compared with `41564767` exactly the 50 notation, tactic, command and metaprogramming declarations are gone and no kind, module, universe list, type or definition body changed; G5, no forbidden name outside the bridge.
- Forward replay, regenerated from the edited shims: the 80 backported laws and the two hand-written checks pass against upstream VCVio `afa6593f`; the control with two altered statements fails at exactly those two.
- Certificates: the stage certificate file has no entry (rc 0). Self-test with the out-of-build tool: 10 of 10 certificates pass (old statement or body equal to the pre-stage dump of `b8db7926` by hash and `pp.all` text, iff or equation proved); 10 twin texts are printed (three with the unused-variable header), inserted into a copy of the self-test module, compiled without the tool with no added warning, and 10 of 10 have the dump's type (eight theorems) or body (two wrappers) by hash and `pp.all` text; the weakened control fails both as a certificate and as a twin; removing the twins restores the file byte for byte.
- Checklist step 7 on this build: the axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95198 reached 122188 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines (the reached set is smaller than at `41564767` because the bridge no longer reaches Lean's elaborator); the source scan printed `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of the three changed modules, the trust-boundary file and `check-inventory.sh`, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00000764): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19 (only Lean files change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: stage S0 with amendment A1 is complete (code, certificates, inventory and documentation); it has not had an independent review and is not integrated into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.


## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S0, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S0 at `c385cfb32d2f8031634ae6196f0f684cb5936278` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-1 to EQ-5) and a hygiene review (verdict: request changes; findings H1 to H6). Both confirm that S0 changes no statement: a binder-aware fingerprint of every declaration before and after the stage shows no type, definition body, universe, kind, module or instance change. Claude fixed every finding on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S0` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s0-review.json`; none needed an owner decision. No Lean file of the build changed, so no statement, proof or axiom guard changed.

Inventory and documents. H1: the amendment A1 zero-count pins of `check-inventory.sh` missed notation with a precedence (`infix:50`, the very line that A1 removed), scoped notation and declarations after `open … in` or `set_option … in`, because they required whitespace after the keyword. They now take the forms of the step 7 source scan, also cover `run_elab`, `run_meta`, `run_tac` and `Lean.Parser`, and new pins allow no instance attribute or derived instance in the five modules and exactly the four relation instances of `EvalDistEq` in `VCVioForwardEvalDist` and no instance in the other four. A control with the exact patterns: the old pin found 0 lines in the pre-A1 `VCVioForwardEvalDist` and 10 of 25 notation probes; the new pins find the removed `infix:50` line, the three tactic and command declarations of the pre-A1 bridge, every one of 25 notation, 11 metaprogramming, 5 instance-attribute and 9 instance probes, nothing in a clean probe, and nothing in the current modules beyond the four instances. H5 and H1: the trust-boundary section now says that 80 of the 83 forward-shim theorems carry upstream names and the three of `VCVioForwardProbabilityTheory` are project lemmas, and states exactly what the inventory pins. H4: the S0 entry above is marked as superseded in part, and the analysis and trust-boundary sections link the A1 entry and this one. H3: the plan copy records amendment A1.1 (the second twin header `set_option linter.unusedVariables false in`, which the tool prints only when a delaborated statement names binders that its text never uses, and how gate G2 and the inventory treat twins), and its status paragraph notes that the owner's revision of D7 removed the `verify-in-shell` runs. H2: the five modules are now registered in the coordination registry (late; the registry is outside the repository). H6: no change; the receipt of `41564767` stays byte-stable.

Certificate tool, outside the build (`/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/tools/CertTools.lean`, now sha256 `01da0e00e6f136e5172dcc734e25dcc64c143066d8a815f9ce96c9344da12cad`). EQ-1: the proof of each certificate's iff or wrapper equation was only elaborated; it is now added as a theorem in a throw-away copy of the environment with synchronous kernel checking, its axioms must be among `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, and each PASS line prints them. EQ-2: a certificate file cannot write a private declaration of another module (20 of the plan's 756 restated declarations are private); the tool, the pre-stage dump program and the certificate generator now resolve a name that is not public to the unique private constant with that user name, and the twin printer refuses private declarations, which have no consumer outside their module. EQ-5: every `pp.all` text of the tool and the dump is printed with `pp.maxSteps` 10⁹, and a text with the omission marker `⋯` is rejected. EQ-3: the declaration dump of gate G4 now adds the review's binder-aware fingerprint of every type and of every definition or opaque body and the instance flag, the dump checks before it runs that the fingerprint separates binders that differ only in name or annotation while `Expr.hash` does not, and G4 compares the new columns. While recording A1.1, Claude found that the S0 gate script recognised only the first twin header and read the wrong field for a twin's name, so G2 would have failed on the first stage with twins; both are corrected and tested on the ten self-test twins.

Phase B exceptions (EQ-4). In the 14 files that S0 edited, 13 theorems whose statements do not use the deprecated interface and that no stage restates still reference the deprecated layer in their proofs; S0 leaves them, like every other remaining use in these files, to the stage that owns the file, and records them here. Eight reach it only by unfolding definitions whose bodies use the deprecated forms: in S5, `CtxComputationalPrivacy.ctxPrivacySecretPrefixProbabilityInner_eq_directSamplePublicPrefix`, `ctxPrivacySecretPrefixProbability_le_booleanINDDollar` and `ctxPrivacySecretPrefixProbability_le_probeINDDollar`, `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar.ctxIndependentPublicPrefixProbability_eq_directSample`, `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner_eq_directSamplePublicPrefix` and `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability_le_modifiedNonceAeadINDDollar`, and `CtxNonceAeadIndDollarValidation.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability_le_booleanINDDollar`; in S8, `PqxdhFirstRecordStopTagged.source_tagged`. Five apply deprecated lemmas directly: in S5, `CtxComputationalPrivacy.ofReal_ctxPrivacyAdvantage_le_viewAdvantage_add_secretPrefix`; in S6, `RawCtxSupportedMixture.bound`; in S8, `PqxdhFirstRecordStopTagged.support_right`, `bind_le_support` and `tagged_game_bound`. The earlier wording that the remaining uses serve deprecated statements was too broad; the analysis section is corrected.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S0/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (lease 00000769): 5,535 jobs, nothing rebuilt, rc 0; 1,338 deprecation warnings of the probability interface, as at `c385cfb3`.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of `check-inventory.sh` and the trust-boundary file, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell, on the edited tree and again on the committed tree: "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Checklist step 7 source scan: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- Certificates with the corrected tool: the stage certificate file has no entry (rc 0). The self-test, now with an eleventh, private subject (`RawCtxFirstParentRisk.uniform_address`, restated privately in a scratch module and certified against the private original): 11 of 11 certificates pass, each kernel-checked with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext`; the pre-stage dump, regenerated with the new dump program, reproduces the earlier ten entries byte for byte; the ten twin texts are identical to the earlier run's, compile without the tool, and have the recorded old types and bodies; removing them restores the file byte for byte. The negative controls produce exactly their nine errors: the weakened restatement is neither certified nor twinned; restatements that differ from the original only in one binder's annotation (`{c}` for `(c)`) or name (`crypto` for `c`) are rejected although the rebuilt old statement has the recorded `Expr.hash`, because the `pp.all` text differs; a private declaration gets no twin; the kernel check rejects an ill-typed proof term and a proof that uses a foreign axiom, and accepts an axiom-free one.
- Gate G4 with fingerprints: dumps of the base build (95,092 declarations) and of this build (95,198); their first six columns equal the earlier dumps, and the fingerprint columns equal the equivalence review's independent dumps for every declaration. No type, definition body or opaque body changed even up to binder names and annotations, and no instance flag changed; G1, G2, G3 and G5 are unchanged. A copy of the stage dump with one doctored type fingerprint and one doctored instance flag fails G4 on exactly those two declarations.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only Lean files and scratch tools change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed; stage S0 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S1 (2026-10-20)

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s1-review-fixes-2026-10-20) below recount the phase B probe over every library (the count below covers only constants of VCVio's core library and `ToMathlib`), give the certificate tool a lemma set for VCVio's native semantics, and correct the wording of the trust-boundary paragraph and of the receipt. This entry remains the record of `c8637a5a`.

At the owner's decision of 2026-10-19 (item 4: plan [`vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md`](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) with D1 to D5, D8 to D11 and phase B of D6; item 5: Lean-only validation) and under amendments A1 and A1.1 of the plan, Claude implemented stage S1 (the pilot of section 7 of the plan) on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S1` in a fresh clone of origin based on central `1da1ebf49f2850a531984ad407a48a834c27fcca` (stage S0, integrated on 2026-10-20), with the Lake packages copied read-only from the S0 clone and the project built in the clone. Commits: `e77a141c` (Lean modules, `check-inventory.sh`, the Lean README, the trust-boundary paragraph and nine rehashed inventory rows) and the commit that adds this entry, the plain-English section [VCVio deprecation migration, stage S1: the CTX reduction wrappers and the VCVio pilot (2026-10-20)](../formal-verification-analysis.md#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s1-the-ctx-reduction-wrappers-and-the-vcvio-pilot-2026-10-20) and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s1.json`.

What was restated (22 declarations, the plan's list). `VCVioFeasibility.ctx_binding_bound_tight`, `ctx_binding_bound_tight_512`, `ctx_binding_bound` and `ctx_binding_bound_512`: `Pr[fun result => result.1 = true | ctxBindingGame adversary]` became `Pr{let result ← ctxBindingGame adversary}[result.1 = true]`. The bodies of `CtxReduction.ctxMisattributionAdvantage`, `ctxFullCommitmentAdvantage`, `ctxRelabelAdvantage`, `ctxWrongSequenceAdvantage`, `ctxWrongSenderAdvantage` and `ctxCrossSessionAdvantage`, `CtxTransitionReduction.beaconWrongSequenceAdvantage`, `beaconWrongSenderAdvantage` and `beaconCrossSessionAdvantage`, and `CtxRetainedTagProjection.fixedMaterialContextCtxFreshOpeningProbability` and `sameViewBaseFreshOpeningProbability`: `Pr[= true | exp]` became `Pr{let x ← exp}[x = true]`. The bodies of `CtxAuthClassification.ctxAcceptedFullFreshForgeryProbability`, `ctxFreshAcceptedBaseProjectionProbability`, `ctxContextAliasReplayProbability`, `ctxNonceConsistentAliasReplayProbability`, `ctxFreshTargetOuterHashAliasReplayProbability`, `ctxNonceConsistentFreshAcceptedBaseProjectionProbability` and `ctxNonceConsistentAcceptedFullFreshForgeryProbability`: `Pr[fun attempt => E | attemptComputation]` became `Pr{let attempt ← attemptComputation}[E]`. The statement text is the output of the pin-filtered upstream codemod, identical to S0's dry run. The definitions keep their names, binders and types; the 14 theorems stated through them keep their exact statements. The reference probe found no reference to the 22 declarations outside the five modules, so S1 has no transitional twin and no consumer patch.

What else was edited. Eleven proofs were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B): `CtxReduction.ctxMisattributionAdvantage_le_blake2b_cr`, `ctxFullCommitmentAdvantage_le_blake2b_cr` and `ctxRelabelAdvantage_le_blake2b_cr`; `CtxTransitionReduction.beaconWrongSequenceAdvantage_eq_zero`, `beaconWrongSequenceAdvantage_le_blake2b_cr`, `beaconWrongSenderAdvantage_eq_zero` and `beaconCrossSessionAdvantage_le_blake2b_cr`; `CtxRetainedTagProjection.fixedMaterialContext_ctxFreshOpeningProbability_le_sameViewBaseFreshOpeningProbability`; and the three probability theorems of `CtxAuthClassification`. They replace `probOutput_bind_mono`, `probOutput_pure_bool_le`, `probEvent_mono''`, `probEvent_or_le`, `probOutput_eq_zero_iff`, a full `simp` over the deprecated body and the simplifier bridge `evalDist_apply_singleton` by a computation equation `exp = (fun a => decide (E a)) <$> adversary` (proved by `simp only` with the experiment's definition, `bind_pure_comp` and `Functor.map_map`) and the named laws `prEvent_map`, `prEvent_mono`, `prEvent_eq_zero_of_forall_not`, `prEvent_eq_evalDist_singleton` and `prEvent_or_le`, without rewriting inside the event notation (plan section 3, check U6). The base proofs of `ctx_binding_bound_tight` and `ctx_binding_bound` are `rw [← VCVioLegacy.oracleComp_probEvent_eq_prEvent_native]; exact binding_bound adversary` (resp. `binding_bound_via_cr_chain`). `VCVioLegacyBridge` gained `oracleComp_probOutput_eq_prEvent_native`, `oracleComp_probEvent_eq_prEvent_native` and `oracleComp_evalSPMF_eq_iff_evalDistEq_native`, stated for `[spec.IsUniformSpec] [∀ t, MeasurableSpace (spec.Range t)] [∀ t, DiscreteMeasurableSpace (spec.Range t)]`, where `Pr{…}` elaborates with VCVio's native free-monad semantics (without the discrete instance it falls back to the `SPMF` adapter), each with its own `set_option linter.deprecated false in` and an exact guard; the `_of_compatible` bridges, documented by S0 for concrete specifications, would have put `PFunctor.FreeM.instDiscreteEvalDistCompatibleOfCompatible` into the pilot's proof terms (a gate G5 forbidden pair; scratch probe). `CtxRetainedTagProjection` and `CtxAuthClassification` import `VCVioForwardOracleComp` (see the next paragraph). `check-inventory.sh`: the bridge's theorem, guard and suppression counts 14 to 17 and six name pins for the new theorems. The Lean README's pilot paragraph and a trust-boundary paragraph describe the stage.

Finding of the certificate run. The first certificate run (on the first full build) passed 13 of 22: the nine definitions of `CtxRetainedTagProjection` and `CtxAuthClassification` reproduced their old bodies exactly, but the proof of `W = old body` failed with two sides that print identically. Probes showed that `Pr{let x ← mx}[p x]` for `mx : ProbComp α` elaborates with `PFunctor.FreeM.instEvalDistSemanticsFreeM` (VCVio's native semantics) in `CtxReduction`, the shims and the bridge, but with the pin's adapter `instEvalDistSemanticsOfMonadLiftTSPMF` (priority 10, defined through the deprecated `SPMF`) in these two modules, whose imports lack the measurable structure of `unifSpec`'s answer types. At the base, only the bridge's three `oracleComp_` theorems used the adapter. With the `VCVioForwardOracleComp` import both modules use the native semantics; the second build and certificate run pass, and the probe of used constants shows the bridge as the only user of the adapter. Every later stage has to give its restating modules the native semantics; the certificate tool rejects a mismatch.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S1/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's): on the unmodified base tree (lease 00000773, 01:47 to 02:09; 5,535 jobs, the base build and base log of gate G1), on the first S1 tree (lease 00000775, 02:10 to 02:31) and on the final Lean sources (lease 00000776, 02:36 to 02:57): 5,535 jobs, 1,856 modules rebuilt, 0 errors, so every axiom guard holds. No Lean file changed after `check-lean-2` started.
- Gate G1, deprecation warnings of the 41 deprecated VCVio declarations at unique positions in project files: 1,338 to 1,316, exactly the 22 warned sites (`probOutput` 11, `probEvent` 11), only in the five files; warning categories against the base log: no category grows, no new kind. Gate G2, lexer scan: 1,724 sites to 1,702 outside the bridge (22 removed, none added), 182 suppression lines (unchanged), no twin. Gate G3, declarations whose statement or definition body uses the deprecated interface: 756 to 734, none in the five modules.
- Gate G4, declaration dump of every `BeaconcryptCore` module with the binder-aware fingerprints, base (95,198, byte-identical to S0's dump at `c385cfb3`, whose Lean sources equal `1da1ebf4`) against stage (95,214): type changes only in the four restated theorems and the equation lemmas `eq_1` of `ctxCrossSessionAdvantage`, `ctxRelabelAdvantage`, `ctxWrongSenderAdvantage` and `ctxWrongSequenceAdvantage`; body changes only in the 18 restated definitions; proof changes only in the 15 edited or restated theorems and the local instance `VCVioFeasibility.instFiniteU8_beaconcryptCore` (type unchanged; `Finite (BitVec 8)` now comes through the bridge's import closure, as for one instance in S0); added: the three bridge theorems, 12 equation lemmas that the new proofs generate (among them `CollisionResistance.crExp.eq_1`) and four `_proof_1` helpers of the new bodies (the `DiscreteMeasurableSpace (unifSpec.Range t)` argument of the native semantics); removed: two `_simp_1_*` helpers of a replaced proof and the equation lemma `beaconWrongSequenceAdvantage.eq_1` that the old proof had generated; no kind, module, universe or instance-flag change, nothing in any other module. The S1 gate script extends S0's with these explanations of generated declarations (configuration flags documented in the script).
- Gate G5: no forbidden name newly referenced outside the bridge; residual pairs of a project module and a referenced VCVio constant absent from upstream `afa6593f`: 3,210 to 3,186, none new. Phase B probe of the constants used by the five modules' declarations, over constants of VCVio's core library and `ToMathlib` only (see the review fixes below for every library): 172 uses of deprecated or deprecated-stated constants in 38 declarations to 6 in two (`ctx_binding_bound_tight` and `ctx_binding_bound`, which consume VCVio's deprecated-stated `binding_bound` and `binding_bound_via_cr_chain` through the bridge, as plan section 5.2 intends).
- Certificates (plan section 5.2) with the out-of-build `CertTools` (sha256 `01da0e00…`, compiled against the stage build; its olean is byte-identical to the shared build): 22 of 22 pass, each old statement or body equal to the pre-stage dump (`base/s1-base.jsonl`, taken against the published `LEAN_PATH` of `1da1ebf4`) by type or value hash and `pp.all` text, each iff or equation kernel-checked with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext`; the four theorems and `ctxNonceConsistentAliasReplayProbability` have no ambiguous event, and the other 17 definitions take the first reading of their one ambiguous event (an output `x = true` read as `Pr[= true | exp]`, an eta-reducible event read as the written `fun attempt => …`). Five negative controls fail exactly at their commands.
- Checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md) on this build: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`; the exact axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95214 reached 122205 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of the nine changed rows, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell: "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only Lean files change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S1, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S1 at `c8637a5ae8a27dd82704a5dd260e928fd2c08ab1` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S1-1 to EQ-S1-3) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 to H4). The equivalence review confirmed the 22 certificates both with the certificate tool and independently of it: it re-elaborated each old form from the source text of `1da1ebf4` and matched the pre-stage records by `Expr.hash`, the binder-aware fingerprint and the `pp.all` text, proved each iff or wrapper equation in a compiled file with only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, and caught a changed event, a binder rename and a binder-annotation change in its negative controls. Its declaration diff agrees with gate G4. Claude fixed every finding on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S1` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s1-review.json`; none needed an owner decision. No Lean file of the build changed, so no statement, proof or axiom guard changed.

Phase B probe (EQ-S1-1). The stage's probe of the constants that its declarations use kept only constants of the module roots `VCVio` and `ToMathlib`. It therefore missed VCVio's separate `Examples` library, whose `binding_bound` and `binding_bound_via_cr_chain` are stated in the deprecated form, and it saw no project constant. Version 2 of the probe sees every root and classifies each used constant as a dependency constant, a constant of the allowlisted bridge or another project constant; the phase B report gives the three classes separately and keeps the version 1 subset for reconciliation. Retaken over the five modules at the base (the published `LEAN_PATH` of `1da1ebf4`) and at this build: uses of deprecated or deprecated-stated dependency constants went from 174 in 38 declarations to 8 in two, `ctx_binding_bound_tight` and `ctx_binding_bound`, each of which uses `binding_bound` or `binding_bound_via_cr_chain` of `Examples.CommitmentScheme.Binding`, `probEvent`, `SPMF` and `SPMF.instMonadLiftPMF`; the roots `VCVio` and `ToMathlib` alone give exactly the earlier 172 and 6. Uses of project constants stated in the deprecated form went from 2 to 0 (at the base, `ctx_binding_bound_tight_512` and `ctx_binding_bound_512` used the two base bounds, then stated in the deprecated form), and uses of the bridge from 0 to 2 (`oracleComp_probEvent_eq_prEvent_native` in the two base proofs). This matches the reviewer's own probe over all roots (10 rows in the same two declarations). The probe of used constants still shows the bridge as the only user of the pin's `SPMF` adapter instance. The analysis section now gives these counts and names the libraries, and later stages use version 2 (`S1/tools/FacadeProbe.lean.in` and `S1/tools/phaseb.py` in the migration scratch directory; version 1 is kept as a provenance copy).

Certificate tool (EQ-S1-2; `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/tools/CertTools.lean`, now sha256 `b737ffda3c81b84ca66c3377a95daa4e0e3fcd89920c14dda5da7955425cf0c5`, previously `01da0e00…`; the earlier source and olean are kept as provenance copies). S1 added the `_native` bridges because at a concrete specification the `_of_compatible` bridges put `PFunctor.FreeM.instDiscreteEvalDistCompatibleOfCompatible`, a gate G5 forbidden pair, into proof terms. The tool's twin printer, however, still tried only the `ProbComp`, variable-specification and compatible bridges, so the transitional twin of a declaration about an oracle computation over a concrete specification with measurable answers would have carried that instance; the reviewer points to two of S2's planned twins, `CtxRomAuth.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability` and `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good`. Gate G5 would have rejected such a twin, so the defect could not have reached a landed stage. The tool now has the native lemma set. The twin printer tries it after the `ProbComp` and variable-specification sets and before the compatible set, and likewise in the mixtures with `ProbComp`. The certificates' closing tactic and the scratch `vcvio_legacy` tactic rewrite with the `ProbComp`, native and compatible sets in that order, and the earlier closing tactic stays as a fallback, so every goal that the tool closed before still closes. In an environment without the native bridges the tactics fall through to the earlier sets. Self-test on the four `VCVioFeasibility` bounds, oracle computations over VCVio's `CMOracle`-style random-oracle specification with `BitVec 512` answers: with the fixed tool, each printed twin is proved by `simpa only [BeaconcryptCore.VCVioLegacy.oracleComp_probEvent_eq_prEvent_native] using @T`, compiles without a message in a module that imports only the build, has the pre-stage type (type hash and `pp.all` text), and its proof term names the native bridge and no `DiscreteEvalDistCompatible` or `_of_compatible` constant. With the earlier tool (control), each twin is proved through `probEvent_eq_prEvent_of_compatible`, and its proof term contains `PFunctor.FreeM.instDiscreteEvalDistCompatibleOfCompatible`, which the check reports.

Wording (EQ-S1-3 and H1, H2, H3). The trust-boundary paragraph of S1 now says that apart from the 22 restated declarations only the types of the four equation lemmas that Lean derives from restated definitions changed, and it links this entry. The receipt `vcvio-deprecations-s1.json` now says that `CtxAuthClassification` adds the import of `VCVioForwardOracleComp`; it had said "instead of `VCVioForwardEvalDist`", which described an intermediate state of the session. The receipt also states the filter of its phase B probe and points to the review receipt. The analysis section now gives the bridge's own count of deprecated source sites, 24 to 28 with its three new theorems (project total 1,748 to 1,730), next to the count outside the bridge. Two of the three new bridge theorems, `oracleComp_probOutput_eq_prEvent_native` and `oracleComp_evalSPMF_eq_iff_evalDistEq_native`, have no consumer in the build yet. They stay, because the certificate tool's native lemma set now uses them for the output and distribution forms of oracle computations over concrete specifications; a later stage reconsiders them if they are still unused. H4 (one transient unregistered file and edits during the base build, both disclosed, with no residue and no effect on the base log) needs no repository change.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S1/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- Certificates with the fixed tool against this build: the stage file's 22 certificates pass with PASS lines identical to those of the validation run at `c8637a5a`, each kernel-checked with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext`; the five negative controls fail exactly at their commands; the log checker passes. The fixed tool compiles without a message, and its olean is identical to the shared build's.
- The native twin self-test and its control, as described above.
- Phase B probe version 2 at the base and at this build, as described above.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of the trust-boundary file, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell on the edited tree and again on the committed tree: "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (lease 00000783): 5,535 jobs, nothing rebuilt, rc 0; the deprecation warnings at project positions are identical to those of the validation run.
- Checklist step 7 source scan: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documentation and scratch tools change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed; stage S1 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S2 (2026-10-20)

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s2-review-fixes-2026-10-20) below reword the sentence on the added imports, qualify the twin check (three twins name instance binders that their originals leave anonymous), add the whole-statement equivalence to the textual fallback certificate of `honest_decapsulation`, and record amendment A2 of the plan. This entry remains the record of `86ab011c`.

Corrected again: [the confirming-review fixes of stage S9](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-confirming-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below found that the full simplifications of `UniformTargetSelection.split` also rewrote the pin's expansion of the event notation `Pr{…}`, against the sentence on what else was edited below; the proof now abstracts the point mass before simplifying. No statement changes.

At the owner's decision of 2026-10-19 (item 4: plan [`vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md`](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) with D1 to D5, D8 to D11 and phase B of D6; item 5: Lean-only validation) and under amendments A1 and A1.1 of the plan, Claude implemented stage S2 (the leaf families of section 7 of the plan) on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S2` in a fresh clone of origin based on central `dfede233c4387789e56ff927de121335e6a1933f` (stage S1, integrated on 2026-10-20), with the Lake packages copied from the S1 clone and the two-file proof flake equal to central's. The base measurements were taken against the published `LEAN_PATH` of `dfede233`: the 15 files have 91 deprecated sites (84 warned, 7 suppressed by 6 suppression lines), exactly the plan's numbers, and the restated set and its 11 cross-stage-consumed declarations are exactly the plan's lists. The base declaration dump is byte-identical to S1's stage dump.

What was restated (42 declarations, the plan's list), statement or body only, with names, binders and axiom guards unchanged. `CtxRomAuth`: `ctxFreshAliasVerifier_le_inv`, `ctxFreshAliasGame_le_inv`, `ctxFreshAliasGame_le_inv_512`, `ctxAliasGameProbability_le_cacheHit_add_inv`, `ctxAliasGameProbability_le_cacheHit_add_inv_512`, `ctxAliasGameProbability_le_secretPrefix_add_inv` and `ctxAliasGameProbability_le_secretPrefix_add_inv_512` (`Pr[fun z => z.1 = true | game]` to `Pr{let z ← game}[z.1 = true]`), and the bodies of the definitions `ctxAliasTargetCacheHitProbability` and `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability`. `JointEagerTable.independent_comm_spmf`, `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.evalSPMF_sample_comm`, `extractUniform_eq_hmacReal`, `first32_uniform` and `hmacRandom_eq_independentRoot`, `RawKeyPartition.sampled_partition` and `partition_continuation`, `PqxdhOrderedChainHybrids.materialChain_uniform`, `fullOutput_observed` and `independentHop_eq_hybrid`, and `LiveApplicationNative.native_observation` (`𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` to `EvalDistEq A B`). `PqxdhChallengeSecretRouting.honest_decapsulation` (`𝒮[decaps] = pure (some secret)` to `EvalDistEq decaps (pure (some secret) : ProbComp _)`). `JointTableFreshCandidates.fixed_fresh_guess_bound`, `optional_fresh_guess_bound`, `fixed_vector_bound`, `completed_result_vector_bound` and `mixed_completed_vector_bound`; `PqxdhMaterialKeyCollisions.material_key_eq`, `material_key_mem`, `sampleMaterials_collision_bound`, `sampleTapes_collision_bound`, `observedTapes_collision_bound`, `observedTapes_collision_bound256` and `observedEager_collision_bound`; `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.zeroSalt_key_probability`; `UniformTargetSelection.chance_eq`; `PqxdhBoundaryDeployment.classical_probEvent` and `PqxdhBoundaryDeploymentLater.later_probEvent` (event probabilities to `Pr{let x ← mx}[p x]`). `StatefulGoodEvent.output_good` and `event_good` and its two private helpers `output_bad_zero` and `output_good_false` (output and event probabilities, including in the hypothesis `agree_good`). `PqxdhPublicKdfCounterexample.publicEvaluation_random_probability` (`Pr[= true | exp]` to `Pr{let x ← exp}[x = true]`).

Transitional twins and consumers. The 11 restated declarations used by later stages (`CtxRomAuth.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability`, `JointEagerTable.independent_comm_spmf`, `JointTableFreshCandidates.fixed_vector_bound`, `PqxdhMaterialKeyCollisions.material_key_mem`, `PqxdhOrderedChainHybrids.fullOutput_observed`, `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.evalSPMF_sample_comm`, `extractUniform_eq_hmacReal` and `hmacRandom_eq_independentRoot`, `RawKeyPartition.partition_continuation`, `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good` and `UniformTargetSelection.chance_eq`) have generated twins `T_vcvioLegacy` right after them (amendment A1: one command each, printed by the out-of-build `CertTools` against the layer's build; three, `event_good`, `chance_eq` and `fixed_vector_bound`, take the unused-variable header of A1.1). The stage files interleave with consumer files, so the work went bottom-up in four import layers, each restated, built, twinned and its consumers renamed before the next. The scripted rename switched 80 references in 50 later-stage files. The definition twin `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability_vcvioLegacy` is the equation with the earlier body: the rename does not apply to a definition whose type users must keep its name, so a separate recorded script (`wrapper_patch.py`, with an exact revert) replaced its five `unfold` sites in `CtxPrefixIsolation`, `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar`, `CtxComputationalSecurity` (two) and `CtxUnclaimedAcceptance` by `simp only` with the twin. The rename script renamed the own headers of the four consumers named `event_good` (`RawCtxFlaggedQueryAgreement`, `RawCtxSelectedParentLive`, `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginMuteAdaptive`, `RawCtxSelectedLiveEvents`); the build exposed it, the headers were repaired, the script was fixed (a bare suffix equal to the consumer's own name is never a reference), and the fixed script run on the base copies of the four files reproduces the repaired files byte for byte.

What else was edited. The proofs of the 15 files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports, explicit shape equations instead of rewriting through the event notation's expansion, and plain simplifier calls on probability goals with the pin's three bridges to the deprecated layer excluded. The uniform marginal used by `first32_uniform` and `material_key_eq` is a local counting fact over finite types in general, because the concrete card arithmetic exhausted the kernel's memory, and it avoids `SampleableType.evalDist_uniformSample`, whose instance argument is a forbidden compatibility link. `honest_decapsulation_program`, `native_eq_real`, `source_eq_native`, `split`, `split_of_eligible`, `sum_eq_card_mul`, `chance_ne_top`, `independent_comm`, `sourceRootAdvantage_le_extract_add_hmac`, `adjacent_bound`, `ctxAliasTargetCacheHitProbability_le_secretPrefixQueriedProbability`, `publicEvaluation_advantage` and `real_sourceRoot_advantage_eq_indCCA` keep their statements and have new proofs. Sixteen imports were added, none removed: the bridge in the 9 twin modules (`StatefulGoodEvent` and `CtxRomAuth` also use it in their proofs), `VCVioForwardOracleComp` in six modules and `VCVioForwardEvalDist` in `LiveApplicationNative`. The six suppression lines of `UniformTargetSelection` and the two boundary modules were deleted, and the two boundary module documents now name the event form. In `StatefulGoodEvent`, over an arbitrary specification with uniform answers, and for `ctxFreshAliasVerifier_le_inv`, over CtxRO with 64-byte answers without a measurable structure, the forward notation elaborates with the pin's SPMF adapter (a probe of every stage module shows the native semantics for `ProbComp` everywhere, with or without the shim import, and the adapter in these two settings); the plan forbids adding the measurable hypothesis that native semantics needs, so these statements use the adapter, their proofs use the bridge's `oracleComp_*` lemmas, and the adapter's users are the bridge and these declarations. The remaining phase B residual is these proofs and the two `CtxRomAuth` proofs that consume `uniformSampleImpl.probEvent_simulateQ`, which has no forward law at the pin: 79 rows in 10 declarations (a probe of every library; 479 rows in 58 declarations before the stage).

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S2, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S2 at `86ab011cd44e93c857ca4f8ad2e16b0385f6e7dc` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S2-1 to EQ-S2-3) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 to H3). The equivalence review re-ran the stage's certificate file on the clean build and confirmed the 42 certificates independently of the certificate tool: it copied each old statement or definition body from the source text of `dfede233` into its module's context, matched it with a fresh pre-stage dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text and the binder-aware fingerprint, and proved in a compiled file the iff of each new and old theorem (the two private lemmas and `honest_decapsulation` as a whole statement included), the equation of each restated definition with its old body, and each old theorem from the new one, all with only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. Its negative controls (a changed bound, an implicit binder, a negated event, an added premise) are caught, and its declaration diff, its probe of the twins and their consumers and its phase B probe agree with the stage's gates. Claude fixed the findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S2` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s2-review.json`, except one part of EQ-S2-1 that would change the plan's final criteria and is left to the owner (below). No Lean file of the build changed, so no statement, proof or axiom guard changed.

Adapter semantics (EQ-S2-1; amendment A2 of the plan, recorded in the plan copy's section Amendments). Five restated theorems, `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good`, `output_good`, `output_bad_zero` and `output_good_false` and `CtxRomAuth.ctxFreshAliasVerifier_le_inv`, are about an arbitrary specification with uniform answers or about the 64-byte random oracle. At the pin their forward notation elaborates with VCVio's SPMF adapter, so their elaborated types contain `SPMF` and the adapter instance although they contain no deprecated statement head, which is what gate G3 counts. Amendment A2 names this class and its handling: adapter semantics, proofs through the bridge, residual for the VCVio upgrade. The gate script now also reports, without a pass criterion, the declarations whose type or definition body references `SPMF` without a deprecated head: 108 at the base and 113 at the stage, the five new ones being exactly this class, each with the adapter instance in its type; the 108 at the base use the non-deprecated SPMF-valued API (`tvDist`, expectations, `boolDistAdvantage`). Every earlier entry of the gate report is unchanged. The review also showed that the four later-stage theorems that use `event_good` for probabilistic computations, three in S6 and one in S9, can apply it, once their own statements are restated, only through the bridge; A2 records the choice that S6 makes before it switches them back from the twin (one pin-only bridge theorem relating the two readings, or recorded phase B exceptions). Pending the owner's decision and not in effect: item 4 of A2 proposes that the plan's final acceptance check count the deprecated statement heads and list by name, with the reason, the statements that reference `SPMF` only through the non-deprecated API or through the adapter, and that the D6 target allow recorded exceptions whose dependency or semantics exists at the pin only in the deprecated form. As written, the final check and the D6 target cannot hold at the pin for this class without weakening a statement or adding an instance binder.

Twin binder names (EQ-S2-2). The twins of `event_good`, `chance_eq` and `fixed_vector_bound` name instance binders (`inst`, and `inst_1` in `fixed_vector_bound`) that their originals leave anonymous, because the delaborator names an instance binder that the statement depends on. Type hash and `pp.all` text are equal, which is the plan's criterion, and the statements are the same, but the binder-aware fingerprint differs. The S2 entry above, the analysis section and the S2 receipt now say so. The certificate tool (`/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/tools/CertTools.lean`, now sha256 `987e93df28f0d50ad616536108c41d2220bd985b4feafffc521899f48ee1660e`, previously `b737ffda…`; the earlier source and olean are kept as provenance copies) reports, as information on every same-type PASS line, the leading binders of the checked declaration against the current declaration by name up to macro scopes, hygiene and annotation. On this stage's run it reports exactly these three twins, with one, one and two differing instance binders, and every other twin and the fallback's copy equal. Its twin printer now first tries each delaboration with the named instance binders written anonymously and takes that variant only if it round-trips like any other text and moves binders only towards the declaration's. Self-test on this build: printed again under test names, eight of the eleven twins are byte-identical to the build's, and the three are printed with anonymous instance binders (`chance_eq` then with the plain header, while `event_good` and `fixed_vector_bound` keep the unused-variable switch for other named binders); compiled outside the build, the three have the dump's type hash and `pp.all` text and the declarations' binders. The build's twins stay as they are; later stages print theirs with the improved tool.

Fallback certificate (EQ-S2-3). The textual fallback for `honest_decapsulation` proved the equivalence of the two conclusions under identical binders and derived the old statement from the new theorem. The certificate file now also proves `(type_of% @honest_decapsulation) ↔ (type_of% @certificateOld_honest_decapsulation)`, the equivalence of the two whole statements, binder by binder from the equivalence of the conclusions, with an exact axiom guard; a control shows that the same proof does not derive `honest_decapsulation_program`. The certificate generator documents this three-part fallback for later stages, and the log checker requires the theorem and its guard for every fallback declaration (with the theorem renamed, the checker fails).

Wording (H1, H2, H3). The [production boundary ledger](production-boundary-ledger.md) now names `classical_probEvent` and `later_probEvent` the event forms and notes that since this stage they are stated in the forward event form. The dated PB-D records [`production-boundary-deployment.md`](production-boundary-deployment.md) and [`production-boundary-deployment-later.md`](production-boundary-deployment-later.md) describe the deprecated form and its suppression before this stage and are left as historical records. The S2 entry above now lists the sixteen added imports correctly: the bridge in the nine twin modules (`StatefulGoodEvent` and `CtxRomAuth` also use it in their proofs), `VCVioForwardOracleComp` in six modules and `VCVioForwardEvalDist` in `LiveApplicationNative`. The S2 receipt's gate G4 now explains that its three `type_changed_unexpected` entries are generated helpers of restated theorems, which the gate explains; the gate script lists such entries under `allowed_generated` from now on.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S2/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- Certificates with the fixed tool against the clean `86ab011c` build: the 53 PASS lines of the stage run (41 inverse-map certificates, each kernel-checked with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext`; the fallback's same-type check; the 11 twin checks) are identical apart from the appended binder information; the new whole-statement iff and its axiom guard hold, with no error or warning in the file; the six negative controls fail exactly at their commands; the extended log checker passes. The fixed tool compiles without a message, and its olean is identical to the shared build's.
- The twin printer self-test, as described above.
- The gate script rerun on the stage's data: every gate passes and every earlier entry of the report is unchanged.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` (no row changed: only documents outside the inventory changed), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell on the edited tree (lease 00000804): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (lease 00000805): 5,535 jobs, nothing rebuilt, rc 0; the warning lines are identical to those of the validation run.
- Checklist step 7 source scan: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documentation and scratch tools change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed apart from item 4 of amendment A2, which awaits the owner's decision and concerns the plan's final criteria rather than this stage; stage S2 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S3 (2026-10-20)

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s3-review-fixes-2026-10-20) below say which of the four generated helpers changed their content and record the second certificate run. This entry remains the record of `08cb87f7`.

At the owner's decision of 2026-10-19 (item 4: plan [`vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md`](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) with D1 to D5, D8 to D11 and phase B of D6; item 5: Lean-only validation) and under amendments A1, A1.1 and items 1 to 3 of A2 of the plan, Claude implemented stage S3 (the three large proof files of section 7 of the plan) on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S3` in a fresh clone of origin based on central `77950cf2fcac55ed52ff275de4afae307659c81b` (stage S2, integrated on 2026-10-20), with the Lake packages copied from the S2 clone and the two-file proof flake equal to central's. The base declaration dump and the base source scan are byte-identical to S2's stage dump and scan.

`jointKdfSingletonProgrammed_tracking_agree_good` (output probabilities); `jointKdfSingletonProgrammed_tracking_tvDist_le_bad`, `hiddenRootProgrammed_tracking_tvDist_le_bad`, `jointKdfSimulate_withQueryLog_probEvent_bad_eq`, `jointKdfSingletonProgrammed_tracking_probEvent_bad_eq`, `jointKdfSingletonTracking_candidate_probEvent_eq_random`, `jointKdfSingletonProgrammed_bad_le_random_candidate`, `probEvent_uniformHiddenRoot_candidateHit_le`, `probEvent_hiddenRootIndependentCandidateExperiment_le`, `hiddenRootProgrammedView_probEvent_bad_eq`, `hiddenRootHiddenFirstCandidate_probEvent_eq_independent`, `probEvent_hiddenRootProgrammedBadExperiment_le_hiddenFirstCandidate`, `tvDist_bind_left_le_probEvent_bind` (also in the hypothesis `hpoint`), `hiddenRootProgrammed_tracking_tvDist_le_programmedBad` and `probEvent_hiddenRootProgrammedBadExperiment_le` (event probabilities). `PqxdhProjectionCollisions`: `evalSPMF_split_uniformJointKdfStream`, `evalSPMF_randomOracle_eq_uniform_prefill`, `evalSPMF_randomOracle_prefetch_irrelevant`, `evalSPMF_fixedJointStreamRandom_eq_eagerCoordinates`, `evalSPMF_projectionCoordUnifiedRandom_eq_uniform_prefill`, `_prefetch_irrelevant`, `_query_prefetch_irrelevant` and `_insert_prefetch_irrelevant`, the seven `evalSPMF_sourceKdfEager_*_bind_eq_deferred` (`project_second`, `root`, `initial`, `step`, `project_first`, `project_nonce`, `query`), `evalSPMF_sourceKdfEagerCoordinate_eq_deferred`, `evalSPMF_sourceProjectionObservedFullRandom_eq_deferred` and the private `evalSPMF_run_jointKdfStreamEagerCoordinateForwardImpl_miss`, `evalSPMF_map_fixedJointStreamRandom_query_eq_eagerCoordinates` and `evalSPMF_map_run_simulateQ_eq_of_query_map_evalSPMF_eq` (also in its hypothesis `hquery`) (`evalSPMF A = evalSPMF B` to `EvalDistEq A B`); `probEvent_projectionObservedCollision_eq_cache`, the private `probEvent_projectionCacheBad32_run_le_sum_aux` and `probEvent_projectionCacheBad12_run_le_sum_aux`, `probEvent_projectionCacheBad32_le_birthday`, `probEvent_projectionCacheBad12_le_birthday`, `sourceProjectionCacheBad32_random_le`, `sourceProjectionCacheBad12_random_le`, `sourceProjectionCollision_random_le`, `probEvent_sourceProjectionCollision_fullRandom_eq_cache`, `sourceProjectionCollision_fullRandom_le` and `sourceProjectionCollision_real_le` (event probabilities; a bare predicate `Pr[E | run]` becomes `Pr{let result ← run}[E result]`); `probOutput_collisionBit_eq_probEvent`, `probOutput_collisionReduction_real_eq_sourceEvent` and `probOutput_collisionReduction_random_eq_sourceEvent` (`Pr[= true | exp]` to `Pr{let bit ← exp}[bit = true]` and event probabilities).

Transitional twins and consumers. The two restated theorems used by later stages, `PqxdhHiddenRoot.tvDist_bind_left_le_probEvent_bind` and `PqxdhProjectionCollisions.evalSPMF_split_uniformJointKdfStream`, have generated twins `T_vcvioLegacy` right after them (amendment A1: one command each, printed by the out-of-build `CertTools` against a partial build of the three modules; the first takes the unused-variable header of A1.1 because its printed statement names the hypothesis `hpoint`; both twins have the binders of their declarations). The scripted rename switched 14 references in eight later-stage files, ten consumer declarations: `PqxdhKemCorrectnessStopped.after_bound`, `prepared_bound` and `stopped_bound`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError.continuation_bound` and `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf.projectedOutput_uniform` (stage S4), `RawCtxSelectedAliasCut.cut_distance` (stage S6), `PqxdhInitialAliasBound.cut_distance` and `PqxdhInitialAliasEvent.bound` (stage S7), `PqxdhDeploymentLaterServerKemHop.real_error` and `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemRawGame.branchRaw_real_error` (stage S9). No stage S2 twin is used in the three files, so no consumer switched back in this stage. The insertion script `twins.py` (scratch tooling) parsed no twin from the tool's output, because the twin header line of `CertTools` gained an `information: binders …` clause in the stage S2 review fixes; its pattern now accepts that clause and still parses the earlier lines, which a rerun on S2's twin log confirms.

What else was edited. The proofs of the three files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports, explicit shape equations instead of rewriting through the event notation's expansion, and no plain simplifier call on a probability goal. Two lessons for later stages: a continuation line inside `Pr{…}` at or left of the column of its `let` ends the computation, so multi-line computations there are parenthesized; and a `calc` step `EvalDistEq _ (do …)` leaves the monad of the `do` block undetermined, so such steps fix it. The uniform-query step of the eager-coordinate law, closed before through `OracleComp.evalSPMF_query`, holds by reflexivity, because a uniform `Fin (n + 1)` sample unfolds to the uniform query `n`. `prefix_tvDist`, `boolDistAdvantage_le_tvDist` and `hidden_suffix_bound` of `PqxdhExtractRomHiddenSuffix` and `tvDist_hiddenRootSharedRandomGame_independentGame_le` and `hiddenRootAdvantage_le_fixedHkdfSha512JointStreamAdvantage_add` of `PqxdhHiddenRoot` keep their statements and have new proofs. Two imports were added, none removed: the bridge in `PqxdhExtractRomHiddenSuffix` and `PqxdhHiddenRoot` (`PqxdhProjectionCollisions` already imported it). The eight suppression lines of `PqxdhExtractRomHiddenSuffix` were deleted. The module documents of the three files now describe the forward vocabulary and, for the first two, the dependencies reached through the bridge, and the docstring of the private transport lemma says that its per-query coupling is exact in distribution. The remaining phase B residual is 103 rows in 13 declarations of `PqxdhHiddenRoot` and `PqxdhExtractRomHiddenSuffix` (a probe of every library; 600 rows in 62 declarations before the stage; 20 further rows belong to the two twins and a helper of one of them): total variation, defined at the pin from the deprecated distributions without a forward counterpart, and the pin's deprecated-stated laws for it and for identical-until-bad, which the proofs reach through the bridge. The adapter's users are unchanged: the bridge and stage S2's `StatefulGoodEvent` and `CtxRomAuth`.

The gate script (scratch tooling) now also exempts the base's own twin sites in G2, compares the tree's twins with this stage's and the earlier stages' (`twins_existing`), and explains generated match splitters like generated equation lemmas.

- Checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md): `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`; the exact axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95234 reached 122208 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines.
