## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S3, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S3 at `08cb87f72564959b8b5d64fb00520b81b3e18cc3` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S3-1 to EQ-S3-4) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 to H4). The equivalence review confirmed the 57 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,237 and 95,234 declarations, equal to the stage's dumps by name, type hash and value hash), copied each old statement verbatim from the source text of `77950cf2` into its module's namespace and `open` context in the stage environment, and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels (57 of 57). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement and each old statement from the new theorem (114 proofs; with the two twins, every `#print axioms` is exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`), and six doctored copies (a weaker bound, another event, an implicit binder made explicit, swapped sides, a dropped premise, another output bit) are all detected. The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, `git diff --check`, the set of changed files, the forbidden-name and suppression scans, the inventory pins, and the agreement of the documents and the receipt with the gate and certificate artifacts. Claude fixed the findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S3` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s3-review.json`, except the part of EQ-S3-1 that depends on the owner's pending decision on item 4 of amendment A2 (below). No Lean file of the build changed, so no statement, proof or axiom guard changed and no module was rebuilt.

Certificate method in the trust boundary (H1). The S3 section of the [trust boundary](../../beaconcrypt-core/proofs/trusted-boundary.md#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s3-2026-10-20) said that every certificate of the stage rebuilds the earlier statement by inverting the vocabulary map. That holds for 56 of the 57. The certificate of `PqxdhHiddenRoot.evalSPMF_first32_uniformJointKdfStream`, the uniform-projection law of HB-51's hidden-root module, is the plan's textual fallback, as the S3 entry above, the analysis section and the S3 receipt say. The section now names the theorem and describes its fallback certificate. The inventory row of the trust boundary was rehashed: `coord.sh inv recount` first (1,987, unchanged), then `coord.sh inv rehash` (`proofs/trusted-boundary.md` `92ea25b66602` to `a5003173a3da`, no other row).

Generated helpers (EQ-S3-2). Of the four generated helpers that gate G4 lists with type changes, only `probEvent_projectionCacheBad12_run_le_sum_aux._proof_1_6` and its `Bad32` counterpart changed their type hash. They are arithmetic side goals of the rewritten birthday proofs, not translated statements: after the same premises on the cache keys, `0 < m → ¬m = m - 1 + 1 → False` before the stage and `∀ j, ¬k + 1 + j = k + (j + 1) → False` after it, as printed by a scratch probe on both builds. The two `_proof_1_4` differ only in macro-scoped binder names, with equal type hash, value hash and scope-erased fingerprint. The S3 entry above, the analysis section and the S3 receipt (a note on gate G4) now say so.

Certificate runs (H3). The S3 receipt's list of logs now also names the second certificate run. Its Lean output equals that of the third run apart from timing, but its log checker failed, because it did not find the fallback's whole-statement iff with its axiom guard in the form it requires in the certificate file. The certificate generator was corrected and rerun, and the regenerated file (sha256 `186c6064…`, the file that the receipt pins) passed in the third run.

Wording and coordination (H2, H4, EQ-S3-3). The analysis section now says that the Lean README writes the collision bound as `Pr[collision] ≤ …`, while the analysis and the trust boundary write `Pr[visible collision] ≤ …`; both are ordinary mathematical notation and unaffected. The coordination registry note of the stage's scratch directory and the migration's progress file (both machine-local) were corrected: S3 switched no stage S2 twin back, and the five restated private lemmas are all in `PqxdhProjectionCollisions`.

Phase B residual (EQ-S3-1). The 13 declarations of `PqxdhHiddenRoot` and `PqxdhExtractRomHiddenSuffix` that still reference the deprecated layer outside the bridge and the twins (103 rows of the facade probe) are justified at the pin but do not meet the literal D6 target of zero outside the bridge. Ten state total-variation bounds, two prove their bounds through one, and one is the identical-until-bad equation `jointKdfSingletonProgrammed_tracking_probEvent_bad_eq`. They are now listed by name, with their constants and reasons, in a machine-local exception list (`/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S3/review-fix/data/phaseB-exceptions.json`, summarized in the review receipt) for the final residual report after S9. Whether the final acceptance check and the D6 target accept such recorded exceptions is item 4 of amendment A2. It remains pending the owner's decision, and nothing in this stage depends on it.

Informational (EQ-S3-4). The generated equation lemmas and the match splitter that the stage removed (`JointKdfProjection.width.eq_1` to `eq_3`, `width.match_1.eq_1` to `eq_3` and its splitter, `HiddenRootCandidateHit.eq_1` and `hiddenRootLoggedRandomRun.eq_1`) had been generated on demand by earlier proofs, and no module generates or uses them at the stage. The twin `PqxdhProjectionCollisions.evalSPMF_split_uniformJointKdfStream_vcvioLegacy` has a generated helper `_simp_1_1` whose type mentions `evalSPMF`. It is counted with the twins, and stage S4, which removes this twin, checks that the helper and the twin's inventory pins disappear with it.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S3/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- Certificates against the clean `08cb87f7` build, with byte-identical copies of the stage's certificate file (`186c6064…`) and negative controls and with `CertTools` `987e93df` (olean `779be810`, equal to the shared build): 59 PASS lines (56 inverse-map certificates, each kernel-checked with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext`; the fallback's same-type check; the two twin checks), the fallback's three guarded theorems, the seven negative controls failing exactly at lines 14 to 20, and the log checker's `RESULT PASS`. The Lean output equals that of the stage's third run and of the validation run apart from timing.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` (the trust-boundary row only), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell on the edited tree (lease 00000819): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (lease 00000820): 5,535 jobs, nothing rebuilt, rc 0; apart from the job counters and the count of edited files at the start, the log equals that of the validation run.
- Checklist step 7 source scan: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documentation and scratch files change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed apart from item 4 of amendment A2, which awaits the owner's decision and concerns the plan's final criteria rather than this stage; stage S3 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S4 (2026-10-20)

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s4-review-fixes-2026-10-20) below name all seven twins with the unused-variable header, add the equation lemmas of the two restated advantages to the type changes of gate G4, say which restated definitions have later proofs that depend on their earlier bodies and why one restated theorem has no twin, and record the certificate run against the final build. This entry remains the record of `c8075ef8`.

Corrected again: [the confirming-review fixes of stage S9](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-confirming-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below rewrite three proofs of the stage whose simplifications with the bind laws also rewrote the pin's expansion of `Pr{…}`: `PqxdhDynamicIdentityRouting.reserved_event_le_global`, `PqxdhJointIdentityEvents.reportingRun_le_extractor` and `PqxdhJointIdentityFreshness.targetEvent_le_jointAdvantage`. The last one used the flattened form `OracleComp.prEvent_bind_mono_of_support` that the lessons below recommend; that form needs the expansion flattened first, which upstream VCVio does not produce, so the proofs now compare events after a common draw with `prEvent_bind_mono_of_forall_le_of_support`. No statement changes.

At the owner's decision of 2026-10-19 (item 4: plan [`vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md`](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) with D1 to D5, D8 to D11 and phase B of D6; item 5: Lean-only validation) and under amendments A1, A1.1 and items 1 to 3 of A2 of the plan, Claude implemented stage S4 (the eleven families of section 7 of the plan, with the merged cycle of the joint identity, dynamic and Phase-1 families) on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S4` in a fresh clone of origin based on central `2ea8fd414f4f2dcc874b9189ce5ebdf42979bf57` (stage S3, integrated on 2026-10-20), with the Lake packages copied from the S3 clone and the two-file proof flake equal to central's. The base measurements were taken against the published `LEAN_PATH` of `2ea8fd41`: the 28 stage modules have 176 deprecated sites, all warned and none suppressed, the plan's numbers; the restated set is exactly the plan's 83 declarations (78 theorems and five definitions), and the reference probe finds the plan's 20 cross-stage-consumed declarations with 46 later-stage consumer declarations in 34 modules (the plan's 45 in 34). The base declaration dump and the base source scan are byte-identical to S3's stage dump and scan. The 28 modules form a 21-layer import chain with later-stage consumer modules between its layers, so the stage was edited and built in six groups bottom-up, inserting each group's twins and renaming their consumers before the next group was built.

What was restated (the plan's 83 declarations), statements and definition bodies only, with names, binders, types of definitions and axiom guards unchanged; (output) marks `Pr[= a | mx]` to `Pr{let x ← mx}[x = a]`, (event) `Pr[p | mx]` to `Pr{let x ← mx}[p x]`, and (distribution) `𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` to `EvalDistEq A B`. `AdaptiveSealKeySelection`: `source_prepared_selection` (distribution); `CtxIndependentTags`: `ctxSplitRouted_badProbability_eq_prefixBad` (event), `ctxSplitRouted_badProbability_eq_real_of_main` (event), `ctxSplitRouted_independent_agree_good` (output), `tvDist_ctxSplitRouted_independentTag_le_prefixBad_of_main` (event); `CtxPrefixIsolation`: `ctxPrefixBadProbability` (definition) (event), `ctxReal_prefixIsolated_agree_good` (output), `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner` (definition) (event), `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability_toReal_eq_tsum` (output); `PqxdhDynamicIdentityPool`: `lazy_eager_complete` (distribution), `sampleSeeds_unused` (distribution); `PqxdhDynamicIdentityReports`: `occurred_probability_le_stopped` (event), `reportingRun_le_extractor` (event), `reporting_advantage_le_identity_count` (event), `reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum` (event); `PqxdhDynamicIdentityRouting`: `dynamic_advantage_le_count` (event), `dynamic_advantage_le_sum` (event), `dynamic_reserved_complete` (distribution), `lazy_eager_history_complete` (distribution), `reserved_event_le_global` (event), `zero_identity_event` (event); `PqxdhDynamicKemJointSource`: `selected_source` (distribution); `PqxdhDynamicKemJointStopping`: `bad_output_zero` (output), `event_good` (event), `history_event` (event), `output_good` (output), `post_event` (event), `pre_event` (event); `PqxdhDynamicKemSource`: `selection_sum` (event); `PqxdhDynamicKemStopping`: `bad_output_zero` (output), `empty_history_event` (event), `event_good` (event), `history_event` (event), `output_good` (output), `post_event` (event), `pre_event` (event); `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf`: `append_uniform` (distribution), `fullExtractUniform_eq_hmacReal` (distribution), `fullHmacRandom_eq_independent` (distribution), `initialPrefix_uniform` (distribution), `joinBlocks_uniform` (distribution), `projectedOutput_uniform` (distribution), `take_uniform` (distribution); `PqxdhJointGapDh`: `advantage` (definition) (output), `candidate_success_eq_advantage` (output); `PqxdhJointIdentity`: `advantage` (definition) (output); `PqxdhJointIdentityEvents`: `occurred_probability_le_stopped` (event), `reportingRun_le_extractor` (event), `reporting_advantage_le_identity_count` (event), `reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum` (event); `PqxdhJointIdentityFamily`: `family_advantage_le_count` (event), `family_advantage_le_sum` (event), `sampleFamily_target_first` (distribution), `targetGame_eq_reduction` (distribution); `PqxdhJointIdentityFreshness`: `freshExecution_le_reductionWork` (event), `fresh_probability_eq_stopped` (event), `global_advantage_le_count` (event), `global_advantage_le_sum` (event), `revealed_fresh_probability_zero` (event), `targetEvent_le_jointAdvantage` (event); `PqxdhJointIdentitySessions`: `session_advantage_le_identity_sum` (event); `PqxdhJointPhase1AcceptanceComposition`: `oneSession_authentication_bound` (event); `PqxdhJointPhase1Reports`: `fieldOrigin_advantage_le_sum` (event), `run_event` (event); `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError`: `continuation_bound` (output), `error_probability` (output and event probabilities), `routed_bound` (output); `PqxdhKemCorrectnessStopped`: `after_bound` (output), `prepared_bound` (output), `stopped_bound` (output); `PqxdhLiveKemReservation`: `complete` (distribution), `unused` (distribution); `PqxdhLiveKemSelection`: `source_target_first` (distribution), `target_first` (distribution); `PqxdhOptionalKemSource`: `HonestKeyCompatibility` (definition) (distribution), `directTargetContinuation_eq_total` (distribution), `sourceContinuation_eq_actual` (distribution); `PqxdhPhase1AcceptanceOracle`: `acceptance_advantage_le_sum` (event); `PqxdhStatefulPhase1Authentication`: `continuationAdvantage_le_bad` (event), `generators_commute_complete_continuation` (distribution), `privateSourcePrefix_bad_probability` (event), `privateSourcePrefix_borrowed_complete` (distribution), `privateSourcePrefix_kem_first` (distribution). The hypotheses `compatible` of `PqxdhOptionalKemSource.directTargetContinuation_eq_total` and `sourceContinuation_eq_actual` change in the same way. In the hash-pinned `PqxdhJointIdentity.lean` only the body line of `advantage` changes; the `SOURCE_SHA256` entry of `proofs/source/getter-profile.py` for it changes from `49f2746c41bf71f3b0e9bb966f4fdfa795d70fca95e11442660717ea9a3e3a6c` to `d9b9c25ac490e14743fe77ebfe6ae476d01fd22b8e5dba763f3e5faacf0c1a37` (owner decision D5).

Transitional twins and consumers. Sixteen restated theorems used by later stages have generated twins `T_vcvioLegacy` right after them (amendment A1: one command each, printed by the out-of-build `CertTools` against the partial build of their group; seven of them, those of `PqxdhDynamicIdentityPool.sampleSeeds_unused`, `PqxdhDynamicIdentityReports.reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum`, `PqxdhJointPhase1Reports.fieldOrigin_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhPhase1AcceptanceOracle.acceptance_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhDynamicKemJointSource.selected_source` and `PqxdhDynamicKemJointStopping.history_event` and `pre_event`, take the unused-variable header of A1.1, because with the plain header the unused-variable linter reported their printed statements): `CtxIndependentTags.ctxSplitRouted_badProbability_eq_prefixBad`, `ctxSplitRouted_badProbability_eq_real_of_main`, `ctxSplitRouted_independent_agree_good` and `tvDist_ctxSplitRouted_independentTag_le_prefixBad_of_main`, `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf.fullHmacRandom_eq_independent` and `initialPrefix_uniform`, `PqxdhDynamicIdentityPool.sampleSeeds_unused`, `PqxdhDynamicIdentityReports.reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum`, `PqxdhJointGapDh.candidate_success_eq_advantage`, `PqxdhJointIdentityFamily.sampleFamily_target_first`, `PqxdhJointPhase1Reports.fieldOrigin_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhPhase1AcceptanceOracle.acceptance_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError.error_probability`, `PqxdhDynamicKemJointSource.selected_source`, and `PqxdhDynamicKemJointStopping.history_event` and `pre_event`. The scripted rename switched 33 references in 27 later-stage files (32 consumer declarations). The stage's files switched back from the twins of stages S2 and S3 (`partition_continuation`, `evalSPMF_sample_comm`, the secret-prefix probability wrapper, `tvDist_bind_left_le_probEvent_bind` and `evalSPMF_split_uniformJointKdfStream`), and the S3 twin of `evalSPMF_split_uniformJointKdfStream`, whose only consumer was `projectedOutput_uniform`, is deleted with its generated helper `_simp_1_1` and its inventory pins (the S3 review's EQ-S3-4). The plan's other four cross-stage declarations get no twin (amendment A3 of the plan, recorded after the stage's reviews). Two of the restated definitions among them, `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage` and `PqxdhJointGapDh.advantage`, and two restated definitions that the plan does not count as cross-stage-consumed because their later users name them only in types, `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner` and `HonestKeyCompatibility`, have later-stage proofs that depend on their earlier bodies, which the reference probe cannot see: a proof over the earlier body of `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner` in `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar`, five applications of a `HonestKeyCompatibility` hypothesis as the earlier equation (`PqxdhCorrelatedRootSource`, `PqxdhPrivateRootSource`, `PqxdhPrivateAcceptedRoot`, `PqxdhAuthenticatedRootSource`, `PqxdhInitialRootEventSource`), unfoldings of `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage` (`PqxdhServerAuthJointIdentityEuf`, `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthInjective`) and of `PqxdhJointGapDh.advantage` (`PqxdhServerAuthAssumptions`, by `rfl`), and six bounds of these advantages by one or their finiteness through the earlier body (`PqxdhPrivatePhase1Prefix`, `PqxdhGeneratedPhase1Prefix`, `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthInjectiveGame`, `PqxdhServerAuthIntermediate`, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineInjective`, `PqxdhServerAuthCommonAuthentication`). The third restated definition among the four, `ctxPrefixBadProbability`, is used later only through equations whose types are unchanged, so it needs neither a twin nor a patch. Claude decided, as an implementation choice, to give restated definitions no twin (a twin of the identity advantage would add, and a later stage remove, a declaration in the hash-pinned file) and to patch these fifteen sites at the point of use, through the bridge or, for the six bounds, with the forward lemmas `prEvent_le_one` and `prEvent_ne_top`; `PqxdhStatefulPhase1Authentication.privateSourcePrefix_borrowed_complete`, whose twin text as printed by `CertTools` names the generated matcher `privateSourcePrefix.match_1` and so draws a linter message in every header variant, is used at its single consumer site, in `PqxdhAuthenticatedRoot`, through the bridge as well; a twin copied verbatim from the earlier source text, which states the same type up to a structurally identical generated matcher, was not tried. The 16 edits are recorded exactly, with apply and revert, in the scratch script `wrapper_patch_s4.py`. Three full builds each stopped at one such site in a later-stage module; the remaining sites were found by partial builds and by a scan for deprecated lemmas near the restated definitions and the definitions built on them.

What else was edited. The proofs of the 28 files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports. Lessons for later stages: a simplification with `bind_assoc`, `bind_pure_comp` or `pure_bind` on a goal that contains `Pr{…}` also rewrites the notation's expansion, so the proofs state computation equalities separately, use `prEvent_eq_evalDist_decide`, or use the flattened forms `OracleComp.prEvent_bind_mono_of_support`; this Lean core deprecates `if_pos` and `if_neg` (use `ite_eq_left` and `ite_eq_right`); and `zero_le` takes no explicit argument at this Mathlib. `PqxdhKemIndCca.oneKey_indCCAAdvantage_eq_branchDist`, `CtxPrefixIsolation.ctxPrefixBadProbability_eq_secretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner` and `tvDist_ctxBeforeVerifyGame_prefixIsolatedGame_le_secretPrefixQueried`, `CtxIndependentTags.tvDist_ctxSplitRouted_independentTag_le_prefixBad` and `tvDist_ctxSplitBeforeVerifyGame_independentTagGame_le_secretPrefixQueried`, `AdaptiveSealMultiKeyPrivacy.real_reduction` and `random_reduction`, `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf.sourceFullAdvantage_le_extract_add_hmac`, `PqxdhOptionalKemSource.honestEncapsulation_no_failure` and `sourceGame_eq_actualGame`, and `PqxdhStatefulPhase1Authentication.continuationAdvantage_le_eufCma` and `privateContinuationAdvantage_le_eufCma` keep their statements and have new proofs. Imports of the forward shim or the bridge were added to 14 stage modules (17 import lines), none removed. In `PqxdhKemIndCca` the shim import first stood before the existing `VCVio.CryptoFoundations.KeyEncapMech` import; that reordered the import closure of `PqxdhInitializerSecrecy` (stage S5), whose `runtimeOfImpl_noFail_bool` then found the instance `IsProbabilityMeasure` through the SPMF lift instead of VCVio's native instance; with the shim import placed after the existing ones the proof term is the base one again. The remaining phase B residual is 124 rows in 16 declarations of `CtxIndependentTags`, `CtxPrefixIsolation`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessStopped` and `PqxdhStatefulPhase1Authentication` (a probe of every library; 873 rows in 105 declarations before the stage; 130 further rows belong to the 16 twins and five of their helpers): total variation, defined at the pin from the deprecated distributions without a forward counterpart, VCVio's identical-until-bad bound and the law relating the Boolean advantage to total variation, which the proofs reach through the bridge; the list is kept for the final residual report and item 4 of amendment A2. The adapter's users are unchanged: the bridge and stage S2's `StatefulGoodEvent` and `CtxRomAuth`.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S4/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's): on the unmodified base tree (lease 00000824, 10:51 to 11:13; 5,535 jobs, the base build and base log of gate G1); with all edits, twins, renames and patches, three runs (leases 00000840 to 00000842) each stopped at one later-stage proof that depended on a restated definition's earlier body and was patched, then rc 0 (lease 00000843, 5,535 jobs) and, after the import reorder in `PqxdhKemIndCca`, on the final Lean sources (lease 00000845, 12:21 to 12:40; 5,535 jobs, 0 errors), so every axiom guard holds; single-file checks and partial builds of the six groups before. No Lean file changed after the final build started.
- Gate G1: 1,027 to 851 deprecation warnings, exactly the 176 warned sites (`probEvent` 83, `evalSPMF` 70, `probOutput` 23), only in the stage's files; warning categories against the base log: no category grows, no new kind. Gate G2: 1,380 to 1,204 sites outside the bridge and the twins (176 removed, none added; twin sites 24 before and 54 after, exempt and counted separately), 168 suppression lines before and after, the twin set equal to S2's 11, S3's remaining one and this stage's 16. Gate G3: 635 to 552 declarations whose statement or body uses the deprecated interface, none in the stage modules; its report-only list of declarations that reference `SPMF` without a deprecated head grows from 120 to 126 by restated statements about total variation, none with the adapter instance.
- Gate G4 (binder-aware fingerprints; base 95,234, stage 95,259): type changes only in the 78 restated theorems, in the equation lemmas `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage.eq_1` and `PqxdhJointGapDh.advantage.eq_1` of the two restated advantages, whose types follow the restated bodies, and in five generated helpers of restated or edited theorems (three of them, `sampleFamily_target_first._proof_1_1`, `target_first._proof_1_4` and the matcher `targetEvent_le_jointAdvantage.match_1_1`, differ only in internal binder names with equal hashes; the other two are the simplification helpers `take_uniform._simp_1_1` of a rewritten proof, with universe parameters `u, v` at the base, `u_1` at the stage, and `routed_bound._simp_1_5` of the patched `PqxdhServerAuthJointIdentityEuf.routed_bound`); definition bodies changed only in the five restated definitions and, by binder names only, in that matcher; proof changes in 137 theorems: 88 of the stage modules, 45 later-stage users of the restated declarations or their twins listed by the reference probe, the three patched later-stage proofs outside that list (`CtxNonceAeadIndDollar.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability_eq_directSampleProbe`, `PqxdhServerAuthAssumptions.serverGapDhAdvantage_eq`, `PqxdhServerAuthIntermediate.disclosure_le_gapDh`) and one generated helper; added the 16 twins and 16 generated helpers; removed the S3 twin, its helper and five generated helpers of rewritten proofs; the equation lemma `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage.eq_1`, generated on demand, moved from `PqxdhJointIdentityFamily` to `PqxdhJointIdentityFreshness`. The gate script (scratch tooling) now also explains a deleted earlier twin (`twins_removed`), treats nested generated matchers like matchers, explains a binder-only body change of a generated helper of an explained declaration and the move of a restated definition's on-demand equation lemma between stage modules, and fails on any proof change outside the expected theorems.
- Gate G5: no forbidden name newly referenced; residual pairs 3,016 to 2,862 (154 removed, none added).
- Certificates (plan section 5.2) with `CertTools` (`987e93df`, olean byte-identical to the shared build): 82 inverse-map certificates pass (77 theorems and the five definitions), each old statement or body equal to the pre-stage dump by hash and `pp.all` text and each iff or body equation kernel-checked with exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`; `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf.projectedOutput_uniform` by the textual fallback in S2's three-part form (its verbatim earlier statement, derived from the new theorem, has the dump's type hash `384519500` and `pp.all` text; the conclusion iff, the derivation and the whole-statement iff are guarded; a control shows that the same proof does not derive another statement), because its earlier statement types the two distributions differently (the projections' `List.Vector UInt8 (JointKdfProjection.width _)` and `RootOutput × RootOutput × NonceOutput`, equal after computing the widths) and the inverse map rebuilds both at one type (first run: hash `2618561016`); the 16 twins equal the dump's old types (type hash and `pp.all` text); the seven negative controls fail; the log checker passes. That second run used the build of lease 00000843, before the import reorder in `PqxdhKemIndCca`; the third run, against the final build of lease 00000845, gave the same Lean output and also passed.
- Checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md): `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`; the exact axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95259 reached 122234 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines.
- Inventory: 77 pins of amendment A1.1 in `check-inventory.sh` (for each of the 11 modules with twins the two header variants, the unused-variable switch only on twin headers and the deprecation suppression only on twin headers; each twin by name; no suppression in the other 17 stage modules); the pins of the deleted S3 twin retired; `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of the 72 changed rows, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00000846), which also runs `getter-profile.py` and its test: "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only Lean files change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: stage S4 is complete (code, certificates, inventory and documentation); it has not had the two independent reviews of section 8 of the plan and is not integrated into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S4, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S4 at `c8075ef8458fc1d5a7bebef704cb704c5416958f` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S4-1 to EQ-S4-4) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 to H4). The equivalence review confirmed the 83 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,234 and 95,259 declarations; the 83 base records equal the stage's pre-stage record, including the five definition bodies), copied each old statement, and each restated definition in full, verbatim from the source text of `2ea8fd41` into the stage environment, and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels, and each definition also by the hash and `pp.all` text of its body (83 of 83; for `privateSourcePrefix_borrowed_complete` and `privateSourcePrefix_kem_first` after replacing the copy's own generated matcher by `privateSourcePrefix.match_1`, which has the same type, value and match information). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement, each old statement from the new theorem and each restated definition equal to its old body (161 proofs; with the 16 twins, all 177 `#print axioms` lines are exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`), and eight doctored controls (a weaker bound, a dropped premise, swapped sides, an implicit binder made explicit, another event and three altered definition bodies) are all detected. Its declaration diff and source diff agree with gate G4 and with the stage's patch and rename lists. The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, `git diff --check`, the set of changed files, the forbidden-name and suppression scans, the inventory pins, and the agreement of the documents and the receipt with the gate and certificate artifacts. Claude fixed the findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S4` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s4-review.json`, except the part of EQ-S4-1 that depends on the owner's pending decision on item 4 of amendment A2 (below). No Lean file of the build changed, so no statement, proof or axiom guard changed and no module was rebuilt; no inventory row changed.

Twin headers (H1, EQ-S4-3). Seven of the 16 twins, not one, carry the unused-variable header of amendment A1.1: those of `PqxdhDynamicIdentityPool.sampleSeeds_unused`, `PqxdhDynamicIdentityReports.reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum`, `PqxdhJointPhase1Reports.fieldOrigin_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhPhase1AcceptanceOracle.acceptance_advantage_le_sum`, `PqxdhDynamicKemJointSource.selected_source` and `PqxdhDynamicKemJointStopping.history_event` and `pre_event`. The tool takes that header only when the plain header draws a message, and it differs from the plain header only by switching off the unused-variable linter. `check-inventory.sh` already pinned the correct count per module; the S4 entry above, the analysis section and the S4 receipt (`twin_headers_with_unused_variables_switch`) named only `sampleSeeds_unused` and now name all seven.

Type changes of gate G4 (EQ-S4-3). Besides the 78 restated theorems and five generated helpers, gate G4 lists, and allowlists, type changes of `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage.eq_1` and `PqxdhJointGapDh.advantage.eq_1`: they are the equation lemmas of the two restated advantages, whose types follow the restated bodies (rule 2 of section 5.1 of the plan). The S4 entry above, the analysis section and a note on gate G4 in the S4 receipt now say so, and the entry and the note name the two simplification helpers among the five generated helpers: `take_uniform._simp_1_1` of a rewritten proof, with universe parameters `u, v` at the base, `u_1` at the stage, and `routed_bound._simp_1_5` of the patched later-stage proof.

Declarations without a twin (EQ-S4-2, H2). Section 5.2 of the plan gives each cross-stage-consumed declaration a transitional twin. The stage gave none to the restated definitions `ctxPrefixBadProbability`, `PqxdhJointGapDh.advantage` and `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage` or to the theorem `privateSourcePrefix_borrowed_complete`, and instead patched 16 later-stage proof sites through the bridge or with forward lemmas. This implementation decision was documented in the stage notes, the S4 entry and the analysis, but not in the plan's amendments, which later stages read as binding; it is now amendment A3 (machine-local, `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/AMENDMENTS.md`). A3 names the four declarations, the 16 sites with their stages (six in S5, three in S7, four in S8, three in S9), the apply and revert script `wrapper_patch_s4.py`, and the rule that each site's own stage rewrites or reverts it in its own vocabulary. That includes seven files that have no deprecated use of their own, so that their stages would not otherwise open them: `PqxdhCorrelatedRootSource` and `PqxdhPrivateAcceptedRoot` (S5), `PqxdhAuthenticatedRootSource` and `PqxdhInitialRootEventSource` (S7), `PqxdhServerAuthCommonAuthentication` and `PqxdhServerAuthIntermediate` (S8), and `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineInjective` (S9). The S4 entry said that all three restated definitions among the four have later-stage proofs that depend on their earlier bodies. `ctxPrefixBadProbability` has none, because its only later user reaches it through equations whose types are unchanged, and the entry now says so. The entry also said that the twin text of `privateSourcePrefix_borrowed_complete` draws a linter message in every header variant. That holds for the text printed by `CertTools`, which names the generated matcher `privateSourcePrefix.match_1`; a twin copied verbatim from the earlier source text, which the equivalence review found to state the same type up to a structurally identical matcher, was not tried. The entry, the analysis section and the S4 receipt now say so.

Certificate runs (H3). The S4 receipt's list of logs named the second certificate run, which used the build of lease 00000843, before the import reorder in `PqxdhKemIndCca`. It now also names the third run, against the final build of lease 00000845, whose Lean output equals that of the second run apart from timing and whose log checker passed. The S4 entry above says the same.

Twin-text runs (H4). While `CertTools` elaborated candidate twin commands in memory, outside the build, Lean's unused-variable linter panicked on some of them (`PANIC at String.Slice.pos!`: an offset not on a UTF-8 character boundary, inside the linter): 48 times in the run of group B1 and six times each in groups D1 and D2, never in stages S2 and S3. The panics have no effect on the chosen headers, which depend only on the elaboration messages, or on the build, whose logs contain none. The S4 receipt (a finding) and the stage notes now record them, so that later stages do not take them for a build problem.

Phase B residual (EQ-S4-1). The 16 declarations of `CtxIndependentTags`, `CtxPrefixIsolation`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError`, `PqxdhKemCorrectnessStopped` and `PqxdhStatefulPhase1Authentication` that still reference the deprecated layer outside the bridge and the twins (124 rows of the facade probe) are justified at the pin but do not meet the literal D6 target of zero outside the bridge: statements and proofs about total variation, VCVio's identical-until-bad bound and the law relating the Boolean advantage to total variation, all stated at the pin only in the deprecated form. They are listed by name, with their constants and reasons, in a machine-local exception list (`/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S4/review-fix/data/phaseB-exceptions.json`, a copy of the stage's list, which the equivalence review reproduced; summarized in the review receipt) for the final residual report after S9. Whether the final acceptance check and the D6 target accept such recorded exceptions is item 4 of amendment A2. It remains pending the owner's decision, and nothing in this stage depends on it. The review's own probe also reported three uses of `evalDist_apply_univ` in `PqxdhKemIndCca`; they are substring matches on `evalDist_apply_univ_le_one`, present since the base, and the stage's facade probe, which matches whole names, reports none.

Informational (EQ-S4-4). `PqxdhHypothesisWitness.toy_compatible` (stage S8), the witness that `HonestKeyCompatibility` is satisfiable, is unedited, but its proof term now refers to the restated definition's new generated helper `HonestKeyCompatibility._proof_1`; its type is unchanged. The generated simplification helpers of the patched `PqxdhServerAuthJointIdentityEuf.routed_bound` changed, and `ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner.eq_1` is now generated in `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar`. All of these are proof-level and listed in the stage's validation; stage S8 should expect the proof term of `toy_compatible` to follow the body of `HonestKeyCompatibility`.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S4/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- Certificates against the clean `c8075ef8` build, with byte-identical copies of the stage's certificate file (`954efee0…`) and negative controls and with `CertTools` `987e93df` (olean `779be810`, equal to the shared build): 99 PASS lines (82 inverse-map certificates, each kernel-checked with exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`; the fallback's same-type check; the 16 twin checks), the seven negative controls failing exactly at lines 14 to 20, and the log checker's `RESULT PASS`. The Lean output equals that of the stage's third run and of the validation run apart from timing.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` (every default row unchanged), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell on the edited tree (lease 00000851): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (lease 00000852): 5,535 jobs, nothing rebuilt, rc 0.
- Checklist step 7 source scan: `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documentation and scratch files change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed apart from item 4 of amendment A2, which awaits the owner's decision and concerns the plan's final criteria rather than this stage; stage S4 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

Second round, 2026-10-20. A confirming review of the fixes at `f58054825a80a9d343410a5e3c43177f974a256c` asked for two documentation fixes, made in the commit that adds this paragraph; no Lean file, inventory row or check result changed. First, the universe parameters of the generated helper `take_uniform._simp_1_1` were stated in reverse in this entry, in the S4 entry and in both S4 receipts: they are `u, v` at the base and `u_1` at the stage, as the declaration dumps of the two builds show (gate G4 lists the helper under `levels_changed`); all four places now say so. Second, amendment A3 is now also in the Amendments section of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md), with the sha256 of its machine-local source, together with the owner's acceptance of item 4 of amendment A2 on 2026-10-20 (item 6 of the owner's decision file of 2026-10-19). That acceptance supersedes the statements above that item 4 awaits the owner's decision: the final acceptance check counts the deprecated statement heads outside the bridge and lists by name, with the reason, the statements that reference `SPMF` only through the non-deprecated SPMF-valued API or the pin's adapter instance, and the D6 target admits recorded exceptions whose dependency or semantics exists at the pin only in the deprecated form, the class of the stage's 16 declarations listed under EQ-S4-1. Checks on the edited tree: `coord.sh inv recount` and `coord.sh inv rehash` (no row changed), `make check-inventory` and `make check-lean` in the proof shell (nothing rebuilt), the checklist step 7 source scan (`LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`), the receipts parsed as JSON, and the Markdown paragraph and link checks; nothing else was run, by the owner's decision item 5.

Transitional twins and consumers. Four restated theorems used by later stages have generated twins `T_vcvioLegacy` right after them (amendment A1: one command each, printed by the out-of-build `CertTools` against the stage build; the twin of `AdaptiveCtxPrefixValidation.independent_prefix_le_indDollar_add_intctxt` takes the unused-variable header of A1.1, because its printed statement names the unused hypothesis `small`): `AdaptiveCtxPrefix.retainedRoutedGame_distance_le_prefix`, `AdaptiveCtxPrefixValidation.independent_prefix_le_indDollar_add_intctxt` and `probEvent_le_add_ofReal_tvDist`, and `PqxdhGeneratedNativeContinuation.nativeContinued_keyfirst`. `twin_rename.py` renamed their eleven references in seven declarations of five later-stage files (`RawCtxSelectedKeyProbes` 3, `RawCtxSelectedLiveEvents` 1 and `RawCtxSelectedNativeFreshLift` 1 of stage S6; `PqxdhAuthenticatedRootSource` 3 and `PqxdhInitialRootEventSource` 3 of stage S7). The stage's own files switched back from the earlier twins they used (`CtxIndependentTags` four, `reporting_advantage_le_identity_sum`, `acceptance_advantage_le_sum` of stage S4; the wrapper twin `CtxRomAuth.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbability`, `JointTableFreshCandidates.fixed_vector_bound`, `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.extractUniform_eq_hmacReal`, `hmacRandom_eq_independentRoot` and `evalSPMF_sample_comm`, `RawKeyPartition.partition_continuation` and `JointEagerTable.independent_comm_spmf` of stage S2), and the ten of these twins that had no other consumer left are deleted with `twins.py remove`, together with their generated helpers `partition_continuation_vcvioLegacy._simp_1_1` and `extractUniform_eq_hmacReal_vcvioLegacy._simp_1_1`; 22 twins remain in the tree (stage S2 seven, S3 one, S4 ten, S5 four). The restated wrapper `CtxNonceAeadIntCtxt.modifiedNonceAeadINTCTXTAdvantage` keeps its name and type and has no twin (A3 item 1(a)); five later-stage proofs of its finiteness through its earlier body (`PqxdhServerAuthCtxObserverEvent.ctxLoss_finite`, `PqxdhServerAuthPublicationLoss.recordLoss_expanded`, `PqxdhFirstRecordKeyBound.keyLoss_ne_top`, `PqxdhFirstRecordConfidentiality.privacyLoss_ne_top` and `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx.ctxLoss_ne_top`, all of stage S8) use the forward lemma `prEvent_ne_top _` instead of `probEvent_ne_top`, recorded exactly with apply and revert in the scratch tool `wrapper_patch_s5.py`; the fifth, `recordLoss_expanded`, was found by the first full build, because the plan's reference probe, which records one edge per declaration and target and prefers the type edge, lists it only as a type-level user of the advantage, and a window scan for facade lemmas near the six restated definitions found no other. The six stage-S5 sites of amendment A3 are done: the bridge sites in `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar`, `PqxdhCorrelatedRootSource`, `PqxdhPrivateRootSource` and `PqxdhPrivateAcceptedRoot` are rewritten in the forward vocabulary, and the forward-lemma sites in `PqxdhPrivatePhase1Prefix` and `PqxdhGeneratedPhase1Prefix` stay.

What else was edited. The proofs of the stage's files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports; twelve proofs that used the pin's simplification bridge `evalDist_apply_singleton` (six explicitly, six through a full `simp`) now use the allowlisted `VCVioLegacy.evalDist_singleton_eq_probOutput` or simplification sets without the pin's bridges. Kernel memory: the group builds D2 and D3 of the adaptive CTX modules (leases 00000871 and 00000872) were ended by global out-of-memory kills of one Lean process of about 37 GiB, which restarted the agent service; after the memory scopes of the coordination protocol were introduced, builds of one module at a time with `LEAN_NUM_THREADS=1` in a scope capped at 12,288 MiB (lease 00000874) located the module `AdaptiveCtxReplayTable`, killed in its scope, and `lean -M7000` checks of variants located the kernel's memory exhaustion at `completedSource_eq_eager` and `completedSource_eq_honestCompletion`, whose proofs rewrote with the generic-monad lemma `prEvent_eq_evalDist_singleton` on concrete games over the table `Suffix → CtxDigest`; the elaborator timed out on the same class of step in `AdaptiveCtxHonestCache.honestCompletion_guess_bound` and `AdaptiveCtxStoppedRaw.cachedGuess_bound`. The proofs (not the statements) now state each generic step as a local lemma over abstract computations and functions, applied with first-order arguments, and generalize the uniform table sample; each of the 25 modules downstream then built alone in 2 to 8 s with at most 3.9 GB. Lessons for later stages: never apply a generic-monad `Pr{…}` lemma directly to games over astronomically large finite types; check such modules with `lean -M7000` in a capped scope before a group build; a multi-line computation inside `Pr{…}` must be parenthesized; a full `simp` on a goal with `Pr{…}` rewrites it into the deprecated layer through the pin's simplification bridges.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S5/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's), every Lean job in a memory-capped scope: on the unmodified base tree (lease 00000859; 5,535 jobs, the base build and base log of gate G1); with all edits, twins, renames and patches, the first run (lease 00000952) stopped at the fifth consumer site and was patched, then rc 0 (lease 00000953), rc 0 after phase B round 1 (lease 00000972) and rc 0 on the final Lean sources after removing one unused simplification argument (lease 00000980; 5,535 jobs, 0 errors), so every axiom guard holds; partial and module-by-module builds before.
- Gate G1: 851 to 585 deprecation warnings, exactly the 266 warned sites (`probEvent` 121, `probOutput` 98, `evalSPMF` 47), only in the stage's files; warning categories against the base log: no category grows, no new kind. Gate G2: 1,204 to 938 sites outside the bridge and the twins (266 removed, none added; twin sites 54 before and 46 after, exempt and counted separately), 168 suppression lines before and after, the twin set equal to the 18 remaining earlier twins and this stage's four. Gate G3: 552 to 431 declarations whose statement or body uses the deprecated interface, none in the stage modules; its report-only list of declarations that reference `SPMF` without a deprecated head grows from 126 to 134 by eight restated statements about total variation, none with the adapter instance.
- Gate G4 (binder-aware fingerprints; base 95,259, stage 95,255): type changes only in the 115 restated theorems and, by internal binder names only, in the generated matcher `AdaptiveRawNonceAead.indexedBeforeVerify_primitive.match_1`; definition bodies changed only in the six restated definitions and in that matcher; proof changes in 174 theorems, 162 in the stage modules and 12 later-stage consumers (seven twin users, five patched sites), none elsewhere; added the four twins and 16 generated helpers; removed the ten earlier twins and 14 generated helpers (among them the on-demand equation lemmas `AdaptiveCtxPrefix.RetainedPrefix.eq_1`, `AdaptiveCtxPrefixValidation.loggedProbeExp.eq_1` and `CtxPrefixIsolation.ctxSecretPrefixQueriedProbabilityInner.eq_1`, which no rewritten proof generates any more).
- Gate G5: no forbidden name newly referenced; residual pairs 2,862 to 2,605 (257 removed, none added).
- Certificates (plan section 5.2) with `CertTools` (`987e93df`, olean byte-identical to the shared build): all 121 inverse-map certificates pass (115 theorems and the six definitions), each old statement or body equal to the pre-stage dump by hash and `pp.all` text and each iff or body equation kernel-checked with exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`; no textual fallback; the four twins have the dump's old types; the six negative controls fail at their lines; the checker reports `RESULT PASS`; the run against the final build reproduces the earlier runs.
- Phase B: package rows 1,709 in 194 declarations before, 404 in 54 after, of which 34 belong to the four twins and one twin helper and 370 to 49 declarations of the total-variation class (statements and proofs about `tvDist`, the identical-until-bad bounds, the Boolean advantage against total variation), recorded by name with their reasons for the final residual report; uses of the allowlisted bridge 13 to 40; the users of the pin's SPMF adapter semantics are unchanged (the bridge, `CtxRomAuth`, `StatefulGoodEvent`).
- Checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md): `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`; the exact axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95255 reached 122227 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines.
- Inventory: 62 pins of amendment A1.1 in `check-inventory.sh` (for each of the three modules with twins the two header variants, the unused-variable switch only on twin headers and the deprecation suppression only on twin headers; each twin by name; no suppression in the other 46 stage modules); the ten per-twin pins of the deleted twins removed and 17 count pins of seven modules set to the new counts; `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged), `coord.sh inv rehash` of the 54 changed rows, then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00000988): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S5, review fixes (2026-10-20)

Two independent reviews of stage S5 at `d5c67b3ba98be71ec3b82bf81c27d33a49b80317` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S5-1 and EQ-S5-2) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 and H2). The equivalence review confirmed the 121 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,259 and 95,255 declarations; the 121 base records equal the stage's pre-stage record, including the six definition bodies), copied each old statement, and each restated definition in full, verbatim from the source text of `c90fad15` into the stage environment, and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels, and each definition also by the hash and `pp.all` text of its body (121 of 121; for `indexedBeforeVerify_primitive` after replacing the copy's own generated matcher by `indexedBeforeVerify_primitive.match_1`, which has the same type, value and match information). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement, each old statement from the new theorem and each restated definition equal to its old body (236 proofs; with the four twins, all 240 `#print axioms` lines are exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`), and ten doctored controls (a weaker bound, a dropped premise, a game substituted on one side, an implicit binder made explicit, another event, swapped total-variation arguments, a weaker `2^-127` bound and three altered definition bodies) are all detected. Its declaration diff agrees with gate G4, and the outside users of the four twins and the five patched sites agree with the stage's rename and patch lists. The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, `git diff --check`, the set of changed files, the Lean diff counts, the suppression scans, the inventory pins, and the agreement of the documents and the receipt with the gate and certificate artifacts. Claude fixed all four findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S5` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s5-review.json`; none needs an owner decision.

Proof step (H2). In the restated `AdaptiveCtxPrefix.retainedRoutedGame_distance_le_prefix`, the step that matches the summands of the two sides ended with `try rfl`, which hid whether the step was needed. Scratch checks of the module, each one `lean -M7000` run in a memory-capped lease, show that it was: without it the goal remains, and its two sides differ only by the event `RetainedPrefix (key, x)` against its definition `∃ input ∈ x.2.ctx.publicInputs, SecretPrefixQuery key input`, which `rfl` closed by unfolding the definition at default transparency. The step is now `dsimp only [RetainedPrefix]`, which names that unfolding and leaves syntactically equal sides, so it no longer depends on a definitional check inside the `Pr{…}` expansion (section 3 of the plan). The statement and the module's axiom guards are unchanged. An environment check with a positive control shows that the step generates no equation lemma of `RetainedPrefix`, and the fresh declaration dump of the rebuilt tree is byte-identical to the stage's, proof hashes included. `make check-lean` rebuilt the module, the 1,256 modules that depend on it and the root, 1,258 modules in all.

Inventory comments (H1). The comment of the S5 block of `check-inventory.sh` said that the restated INT-CTXT advantage has four later-stage finiteness proofs; there are five, as the S5 entry above, the S5 receipt and the analysis say, because the comment was generated before the first full build found the fifth. It now says five, and its generator among the stage's scratch tools is fixed and reproduces the block. The S2 to S5 comments ended by saying that the twin pins are removed together with the twins; when stages S4 and S5 deleted twins they removed the per-twin pins and kept the module count pins at the new counts, zero where no twin remains, with a removal note, and the four comments now say so. Only these four comment lines changed; no pin changed.

Reference probe and the fifth finiteness proof (EQ-S5-1). The plan's reference probe records one edge per declaration and target and prefers the type edge. `PqxdhServerAuthPublicationLoss.recordLoss_expanded`, whose statement and proof both mention `modifiedNonceAeadINTCTXTAdvantage`, therefore appears in it only as a type-level user, and the stage's consumer list, taken from the probe's proof edges, had four of the five finiteness proofs; the first full build found the fifth. The S5 entry above and the analysis now say so, instead of saying that the probe did not record it. The result was correct: the review found exactly the twelve recorded later-stage proof changes and no other proof that depends on a restated definition's earlier body. For stages S7 and S8, which restate two and three further definitions, the stage notes and the migration's progress file now ask to take the users of a restated definition's earlier body from proof edges recorded separately from type edges, as the review's environment probe does, before the first full build.

Phase B exception tool (EQ-S5-2). The scratch tool that writes the stage's phase B exception list accepted two names that section 3 of the plan forbids, the pin's simplification bridge `evalDist_apply_singleton` and the instance `PFunctor.FreeM.instDiscreteEvalDistCompatibleOfCompatible`, as admissible parts of a total-variation exception; neither occurs at `d5c67b3b`, and gate G5 checks forbidden names on its own. The tool now fails on any name of the plan's forbidden list, on the pin's three simplification bridges and on any `DiscreteEvalDistCompatible` or `SPMF.toMeasure` name. Rerun on the stage's phase B report and on the report of the rebuilt tree, it reproduces the exception list byte for byte (49 declarations, 370 rows), and three doctored reports, each with one such name added, now fail; the earlier tool accepted two of them.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S5/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's), on the edited tree (lease 00001018, every Lean job in a memory-capped scope): 5,535 jobs, 1,258 modules rebuilt, rc 0, no error, so every axiom guard holds; its 3,494 warning records equal those of the stage's final build and of the validation run, and the warning categories against the base build pass as before (VCVio deprecation warnings 851 to 585).
- Post-build measurements on the rebuilt tree (leases 00001019 to 00001023): the declaration dump, the facade probe, the usage and used probes and the source scan are byte-identical to the stage's; checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md): `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`, and the exact axiom walk printed `AXIOM-WALK roots 95255 reached 122227 unresolved 0` with exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound` and `propext` and unchanged `SERVER-DOMAIN` lines.
- Gates G1 to G5 rerun on these inputs: all pass, with results equal to the stage's; the proof changes are the same 174 theorems; the phase B report and the exception list written by the corrected tool are byte-identical to the stage's.
- Certificates (leases 00001024 and 00001025), with byte-identical copies of the stage's certificate file and negative controls and `CertTools` `987e93df` (olean `779be810`, equal to the shared build): 125 PASS lines (the 121 inverse-map certificates, each kernel-checked with exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, and the four twin checks), the six negative controls failing exactly at lines 12 to 17, and the log checker's `RESULT PASS`. The Lean output equals that of the stage's third run and of the validation run apart from timing.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged) before `coord.sh inv rehash` (`AdaptiveCtxPrefix.lean` and `check-inventory.sh` changed; `BeaconcryptCore.lean` and `trusted-boundary.md` unchanged), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00001026): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only Lean files, the inventory and documentation change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-20: both reviews' findings are fixed; stage S5 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

Twenty-two restated theorems used by later stages have generated twins `T_vcvioLegacy` right after them (amendment A1: one command each, printed by the out-of-build `CertTools` against the stage build; nine take the unused-variable header of A1.1, because their printed statements name binders that the statements do not use: `RawCtxBadEventMixture.bind_bound`, `RawCtxFirstParentRisk.callback_risk`, `RawCtxFlaggedQueryAgreement.event_good`, `RawCtxScaledBadEventMixture.bind_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedKeyProbes.candidate_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedParentCandidates.real_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedParentCandidates.uniform_bound`, `RawCtxSelectiveSingleDraw.single_uniform`, `RawCtxStatefulScanRisk.scan_risk`): `RawCtxBadEventMixture.bind_bound`, `RawCtxFirstParentRisk.callback_risk`, `RawCtxFirstParentRisk.full_inputs`, `RawCtxFlaggedQueryAgreement.event_good`, `RawCtxFlaggedQueryTrace.distance`, `RawCtxInitialChildSecrecy.joint_separated`, `RawCtxInitialChildSecrecy.parts_observed`, `RawCtxInitialSentinelSampler.initial_observed`, `RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.complete`, `RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.source_game`, `RawCtxScaledBadEventMixture.bind_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedAliasInitial.recorded_distance_initial`, `RawCtxSelectedInitialSecond.second_observed`, `RawCtxSelectedInputCandidates.uniform_candidates`, `RawCtxSelectedKeyProbes.candidate_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedMessageKey.key_observed`, `RawCtxSelectedParentCandidates.real_bound`, `RawCtxSelectedParentCandidates.uniform_bound`, `RawCtxSelectiveSingleDraw.single_uniform`, `RawCtxSelectiveTargets.event_partition`, `RawCtxSendLineageCollision.initial_alias_uniform`, `RawCtxStatefulScanRisk.scan_risk`. `twin_rename.py` renamed their 57 references in 36 later-stage files (stages S7, S8 and S9). The stage's files switched back from ten earlier twins to the restated theorems (S2: `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good`, `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.evalSPMF_sample_comm`, `PqxdhOrderedChainHybrids.fullOutput_observed`, `JointEagerTable.independent_comm_spmf`, `PqxdhMaterialKeyCollisions.material_key_mem`; S3: `PqxdhHiddenRoot.tvDist_bind_left_le_probEvent_bind`; S4: `PqxdhFullSecretInputKdf.initialPrefix_uniform`; S5: `AdaptiveCtxPrefixValidation.probEvent_le_add_ofReal_tvDist` and `independent_prefix_le_indDollar_add_intctxt`, `AdaptiveCtxPrefix.retainedRoutedGame_distance_le_prefix`); the five of these without a remaining user, `independent_comm_spmf`, `material_key_mem` and the three of stage S5, are deleted with one generated helper and their pins. Amendment A2 item 3 (implementation decision of this stage): option (b), the three consumers `RawCtxFlaggedQueryAgreement.event_good`, `RawCtxSelectedParentLive.event_good` and `RawCtxSelectedLiveEvents.event_good` apply the restated `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good` (adapter semantics, `spec' := unifSpec`) through `VCVioLegacy.oracleComp_probEvent_eq_prEvent` and `VCVioLegacy.probEvent_eq_prEvent` in both directions; no bridge theorem is added; the twin `event_good_vcvioLegacy` stays for its stage S9 user.

What else was edited. The `𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` equations were converted from their exact bracket delimiters before the plan's codemod, which then rewrote the probability notation; the proofs of the stage's files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports; three proofs exclude the pin's simplification bridges `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ` from their full `simp` and `simpa` calls: `RawCtxSelectedAliasCompiler.source_distance` and `RawCtxStoppedParentSecrecy.local_bound`, which the facade probe of the first passing full build found reaching `evalDist_apply_singleton`, and `RawCtxSelectiveTargets.event_partition`, whose rewritten proof carries the exclusion in three `simp` calls. Elaboration: general monad laws applied to concrete games over uniform 76-, 64- and 32-byte draws made the elaborator stop at its recursion limit (no memory runaway; every check stayed at 3.0 to 4.2 GB in its scope); those proofs now use local lemmas over abstract computations applied with plain arguments or generalize the uniform draw before rewriting (`RawCtxFullPreimageTester`, `RawCtxSelectedParentHop`, the route proofs of `RawCtxCheckedParentSecrecy`, `RawCtxFamilyParentSecrecy` and `RawCtxStoppedParentSecrecy`, `RawCtxSendLineageCollision`, `RawCtxInitialSendAlias`).

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S6, review fixes (2026-10-21)

Two independent reviews of stage S6 at `ed682b31b8807f21ab5153fa4f1fc3836812b1cc` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S6-1 and EQ-S6-2) and a hygiene review (verdict: approve with nits; findings H1 and H2). The equivalence review confirmed the 156 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,255 and 95,253 declarations; the 156 base records equal the stage's pre-stage record), copied each old statement verbatim from the source text of `94066833` into the stage environment, with a header extractor that handles the `let` bindings of S6 statements, and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels (156 of 156, including the textual-fallback theorem `RawCtxPendingChildRealization.random` and the two `agree_good` statements with the type ascription). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement and derived each old statement from the new theorem (312 proofs; with the 22 twins, all 334 `#print axioms` lines are exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`). All ten doctored old statements were detected by the comparison (an implicit binder made explicit, which keeps `Expr.hash`, by the `pp.all` text and the binder list), and the closing tactic refused the other nine (two changed bounds, two dropped premises, two flipped outputs, a negated event, swapped `tvDist` arguments and a changed depth in `random`). Its own declaration diff found type changes only in the 156 restated theorems and four generated `_simp` helpers of restated proofs, no definition-body change, proof changes only in the 167 stage theorems and the 57 later-stage twin consumers, the 22 twins as exact copies of their originals' base types, and its own phase B probe reproduced the exception list exactly. The hygiene review found the branch, footers, whitespace, inventory (2,407 rows, 0 mismatches; the whole VCVio region of `check-inventory.sh` rerun with 0 failures), hash-pinned and generated files, twins, receipt hashes and registrations in order. No finding concerns the meaning of a statement or proof, none needs an owner decision, and no Lean file, inventory row or check script changed in the fixes.

Phase B exception reasons (EQ-S6-1). The scratch tool that writes the stage's phase B exception list gave the reason "proof through total variation `tvDist`" to every declaration whose proof names an `SPMF` constant and whose statement is not about `tvDist`, because its set of total-variation constants also contained `SPMF` and `SPMF.instMonadLiftPMF`. Four of the 39 declarations use no total-variation constant: the three event-agreement consumers `RawCtxFlaggedQueryAgreement.event_good`, `RawCtxSelectedParentLive.event_good` and `RawCtxSelectedLiveEvents.event_good`, whose `SPMF` rows come from the pin's adapter semantics of `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good`, and `RawCtxSelectedLiveTrace.exposure_probability`, whose `SPMF` rows come from the statement of VCVio's identical-until-bad lemma `probEvent_output_bad_eq'`. The tool now keeps total variation apart from the `SPMF` type and its instances, gives the total-variation reason only where `tvDist` or a lemma about it occurs, attributes the `SPMF` rows of the other declarations to their actual source, and fails when an `SPMF` row has neither, or when a statement mentions `SPMF` without `tvDist`. Rerun on the stage's phase B report it writes the same 39 declarations and 259 rows, and only the reason lists of these four change; four doctored reports behave as required (an `SPMF`-only proof without any of these sources and an `SPMF` statement without `tvDist` fail, the plan's forbidden simplification bridge still fails, and a declaration that also uses `tvDist` keeps the total-variation reason), and the earlier tool accepted the first two. The corrected list is part of the [review receipt](proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s6-review.json); the stage receipt keeps the list of `ed682b31` and points to it. Stage S5's list, written by the same rule, has the same wrong reason for five declarations that use `probEvent_output_bad_eq'` without total variation (`AdaptiveCtxAliasCoupling.flagged_bad_eq`, `AdaptiveCtxPrefix.flaggedGame_bad_eq`, `AdaptiveRawNonceAead.adaptiveGame_bad_eq`, `CtxComputationalPrivacy.ctxPrivacyIndependent_badProbability_eq_secretPrefix`, `CtxNonceAeadIndDollar.ctxIndependent_badProbability_eq_prefixBad`); their corrected reasons are recorded in the same receipt for the final residual report, and the lists of stages S0, S3 and S4 have no such entry.

Simplification bridges (H2). The S6 entry above and the analysis said that two proofs exclude the pin's simplification bridge; the exclusion of `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ` also sits in three `simp` calls of the rewritten proof of `RawCtxSelectiveTargets.event_partition`, whose file had no such exclusion before the stage. Both now name the three declarations. Section 3 of the plan recommends exactly this exclusion; no proof changed.

Twin consumers outside the plan's file lists (EQ-S6-2, H1). A scan of the whole tree at `ed682b31` finds 140 references to the 39 transitional twins in 74 files outside their definitions, all in files of stages S7, S8 and S9 of `plan/stage-files.tsv` except four files that have no deprecated use of their own and are therefore in no stage's file list: `PqxdhFirstSharedMessagePrefix` (one reference to `RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.complete_vcvioLegacy`, family `PqxdhFirst*`, switched back in stage S8), `PqxdhLaterSharedMessagePrefix` (`RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.complete_vcvioLegacy`) and `PqxdhLaterExecutableCompletion` (`RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.source_game_vcvioLegacy`), family `PqxdhLater*`, switched back in stage S9, and `PqxdhKeyScheduleFromRoot` (two references to the S2 twin `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.evalSPMF_sample_comm_vcvioLegacy` since stage S2; its family `PqxdhKeySchedule*` is in no stage of section 7 of the plan), switched back no later than the final sweep of stage S9. The S6 tree is correct (every rename is exact and every twin has users); this is the same gap that item 3 of amendment A3 closed for the files without deprecated uses that hold sites of the S4 patches. The stage notes and the migration's progress file now name these owners, and each later stage takes its switch-back set from the references to the twins in the whole tree, as S6 did for the twins it removed, not only from its plan file list.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S6/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- No Lean file changed, so no module was rebuilt and the certificates were not rerun; the `make check-lean` runs, the certificates, the gates and the step 7 scans of `ed682b31` (S6 entry above) stand for this commit.
- The corrected exception tool on the stage's phase B report: 39 declarations, 259 rows, equal to the stage's list apart from the reason lists of the four declarations above; four doctored reports as described; the corrected rule checked against the lists of stages S0, S3, S4 and S5.
- The whole-tree scan of twin references above.
- `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's) on the edited tree (lease 00001140): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary"; no inventory row changed, so no `coord.sh inv recount` or `inv rehash` was needed.
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documentation and scratch tooling change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-21: both reviews' findings are fixed; stage S6 is ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

What else was edited. The `𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` equations were converted from their exact bracket delimiters before the plan's codemod, which then rewrote the probability notation, and three multi-line equations were laid out again so that their `do` blocks keep their columns; the proofs of the stage's files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports, the route and freshness proofs following the templates of stage S6; `PqxdhInitialFutureInputCandidates.clear_bound` and `PqxdhInitialSampledParent.local_bound`, which the facade probe of the first full build found reaching `evalDist_apply_singleton` through full `simp`, now avoid it, `clear_bound` with `simp only` and `local_bound` by excluding `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ` from its full `simp`; the rewritten proofs of eight further declarations exclude the same three bridges from their full `simp` calls: `PqxdhAuthenticatedRoot.dispatch_finish_eq_source`, `PqxdhAuthenticatedRootSource.dispatch_complete_eq_source` and `PqxdhInitialRootEventSource.dispatch_complete_eq_source` (three calls each), `PqxdhAuthenticatedRootCorrectness.after_bound`, `PqxdhComposition.finiteHybridBound`, `PqxdhInitialCommonSecrecy.local_bound`, `PqxdhInitialHalfSecrecy.local_bound` and `PqxdhInitialNativeRisk.callback_risk`, nine declarations with the exclusion in all. Elaboration: general monad laws applied to concrete games over the uniform 76-byte draw stopped the elaborator at its recursion or heartbeat limit (no memory runaway; every scoped check stayed at 3.3 to 4.3 GB); those proofs now generalize the uniform draw before rewriting (`PqxdhInitialPreparedResources`, `PqxdhInitialPreparedRisk`, `PqxdhInitialSourceLaterMixture`, `PqxdhInitialFutureInputCandidates`), and `PqxdhInitialSourceLaterSecrecy.normalized_bound` generalizes its repeated event probability, which brought the module from over 60 seconds to 10.
