## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S7, review fixes (2026-10-21)

Two independent reviews of stage S7 at `d912a809835d8e5ca7d37f82dc59f14a0a7dabd7` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; finding EQ-S7-1) and a hygiene review (verdict: request changes; findings H1, the same defect as EQ-S7-1, and H2). The equivalence review confirmed the 93 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,253 and 95,209 declarations; the 93 base records equal the stage's pre-stage record, including the two wrapper bodies), copied the old statement of each of the 91 restated theorems and the old body of each of the two restated wrappers verbatim from the source text of `6ae77bba` into the stage environment, with a header extractor that now also handles `local notation`, `include ... in` and file-level `set_option` lines, and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels, and each wrapper also by the hash and `pp.all` text of its body (93 of 93, including the textual-fallback theorem `PqxdhInitialNativeChildDraw.bare_uniform`). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement, each old statement from the new theorem and each restated wrapper equal to its old body (184 proofs; with the four twins, all 188 `#print axioms` lines are exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`), and twelve doctored controls (a weaker bound, a flipped Boolean in an event, a swapped bound event in a private theorem, a smaller finite sum, an implicit binder made explicit, swapped key and chain, a flipped output in a loss term, two dropped premises, a flipped output and a shorter sum in the two wrapper bodies and a changed depth in `bare_uniform`) are all detected. Its declaration diff agrees with gate G4, the later-stage users of the four twins agree with the stage's rename list apart from EQ-S7-1, and its phase B probe reproduces the stage's exception list. The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, `git diff --check`, the set of changed files, the Lean diff counts, the residual and suppression scans, the twins, the inventory pins and digests, and the agreement of the documents and the receipt with the gate, certificate and inventory artifacts. Claude fixed all three findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S7` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s7-review.json`; none needs an owner decision.

Docstring word and reference count (EQ-S7-1, H1). `twin_rename.py apply`, which points the later-stage proofs at the twins, had also renamed the English word "complete" in the docstring of `PqxdhAdaptiveMessageKeySource.later_bound` to `complete_vcvioLegacy`: the docstring sat in the text chunk of the consumer `first_source`, and the tool replaced the last name component `complete` of `PqxdhFinalMaterialKeySplit.complete` on every line of that chunk. No type, value, axiom guard or warning was affected, but the S7 entry above, the analysis and the S7 receipt counted the word as a reference. The word is restored, so `PqxdhAdaptiveMessageKeySource.lean` differs from `6ae77bba` only by its two code references, and the six later-stage files hold eight renamed references (stage S8: `PqxdhAdaptiveMessageKeySource` two, `PqxdhHeadlineBridges` two, `PqxdhFirstRecordTarget`, `PqxdhHypothesisWitness` and `PqxdhServerAuthCtxMaterial` one each; stage S9: `PqxdhLaterCommonRisk` one), one in each of the eight later-stage proofs that used the four twinned theorems before the stage and that gate G4 counts as changed. The S7 entry, the analysis and the S7 receipt now say eight; the message of commit `1ab1a706` says 9 and is not amended. The tool now edits code text only: comments, docstrings and string and character literals are blanked with the stripper of the step 7 source scan before matching, and matches inside them are listed instead of changed, both when renaming and when switching back; command heads are read from the blanked text. Replayed on the inputs of stages S5, S6 and S7, the corrected tool writes the same files as the earlier tool for S5 (11 references) and S6 (57), and for S7 the eight code references with the docstring unchanged, equal to this tree; switching back its S7 output restores the base sources byte for byte, and in a synthetic file with the twin's name in line comments, a docstring, a nested block comment and a string literal it renames only the code references. A new check among the stage tools, run after each build, reports any twin name inside a comment, docstring or literal of the tracked Lean files; it finds none in this tree and the one above in the files of `d912a809`.

Simplification bridges (H2). The analysis said that the two proofs which the facade probe of the first full build found reaching the pin's simplification bridge from point masses "now exclude it"; `PqxdhInitialFutureInputCandidates.clear_bound` uses `simp only` instead, and only `PqxdhInitialSampledParent.local_bound` excludes the bridges. The exclusion of `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ` also sits in the hand-rewritten proofs of eight further declarations, 15 `simp` calls in nine declarations in all, none of them in the stage's files before the stage. The analysis and the S7 entry above now say how each of the two proofs avoids the bridge and name all nine declarations. Section 3 of the plan recommends exactly this exclusion; no proof changed.

What else was edited. The `𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` equations were converted from their exact bracket delimiters before the plan's codemod, which then rewrote the probability notation, and all 116 suppression lines were deleted; the proofs of the stage's files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports. Where an unnamed `rw [bind_assoc]` or `rw [bind_pure_comp]` would also rewrite the expansion of `Pr{…}` (the outermost bind of `𝒟[do let x ← mx; pure (p x)] {True}`), the proofs state proof-local event-level laws over abstract computations (reassociation, a final map, a rejected branch, a congruence of binds) and rewrite with those. The facade probe of the build of the second full check found twelve declarations reaching `evalDist_apply_singleton`, and through it `probOutput` and `PFunctor.FreeM.instDiscreteEvalDistCompatibleOfCompatible`, through full `simp` (`PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx.observe_le`; `PqxdhFirstRecordConfidentiality.bit_bounds`, `insSeparated_le_client`, `insSeparated_le_compiled`, `stop_real`; `PqxdhFirstRecordFinishBound.coll_real`; `PqxdhFirstRecordKeyBound.key_real`, `separated_le_source`; `PqxdhFirstRecordTarget.after_split`, `ideal_split`, `next_split`; `PqxdhServerAuthIntermediate.collision_le_scalar_spread`); their seventeen `simp`/`simpa` calls now exclude `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ`. The hand-rewritten proof of `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx.alias_bound` excludes the same three bridges in its one `simp` call, so thirteen declarations of the stage, with eighteen calls, carry the exclusion; none of the stage's files had it before the stage. Elaboration: in `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx.alias_bound` a general monad law applied to the game over `$ᵗ HmacBlock` stopped the elaborator at its recursion limit; that proof and `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCanonical.prelude_alias` now use an abstract computation step and `generalize` the uniform draw before rewriting. `PqxdhServerAuthPublicationGuess.scrub_capture_bound` carries `set_option maxHeartbeats 400000 in` (a scoped check fails at 200,000 at its bind congruence and passes at 400,000; the module checks in 12 seconds). No memory runaway: every scoped single-file check stayed between 3.2 and 4.6 GiB.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S8/logs/`). All passed.

- Gate G1: 65 to 36 deprecation warnings, exactly the 29 warned sites (`probOutput` 13, `probEvent` 12, `evalSPMF` 4), only in the stage's files; warning categories against the base log: no category grows, no new kind. Gate G2: 418 to 146 sites outside the bridge and the twins (272 removed, none added; twin sites 52 before and 49 after, exempt and counted separately), 168 suppression lines before and 52 after, the twin set equal to the 16 remaining earlier twins and this stage's seven. Gate G3: 182 to 54 declarations whose statement or body uses the deprecated interface, none in the 76 stage modules; its report-only list of declarations that reference `SPMF` without a deprecated head stays at 163.

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S8, review fixes (2026-10-21)

Corrected in part: [the closing checks of phase B](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-closing-checks-of-phase-b-stage-pb-2026-10-21) below make precise what the moved comment of `PqxdhServerAuthCollision` kept (finding CONF-S8-1 of the confirming review of these fixes): the file's line count and every declaration position, while three lines inside the proof of `verified_third_probability` moved up by one. Where the paragraph on proof comments and the `make check-lean` bullet below say that the edit keeps every position or every line, read this; the reuse of the nineteen modules that import it is unaffected. This entry remains the record of `86bbc791`.

Two independent reviews of stage S8 at `f752e169eb227a3d5312403096f59d7ab116869a` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; findings EQ-S8-1 and EQ-S8-2) and a hygiene review (verdict: request changes; findings H1, the same defect as EQ-S8-1, H2, which includes EQ-S8-2, H3 and H4). The equivalence review confirmed the 128 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh declaration dumps of the base and stage builds (95,209 and 95,184 declarations; the 128 base records equal the stage's pre-stage record, including the three wrapper bodies), copied the old statement of each of the 125 restated theorems and the old body of each of the three restated wrappers verbatim from the source text of `326d5b85` into the stage environment, with a header extractor that now tracks namespaces across modules (the namespace `PqxdhServerAuthEntryFactor` spans six of them), and matched each copy with the fresh base dump by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased binder-aware fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels, and each wrapper also by the hash and `pp.all` text of its body (128 of 128, including `PqxdhServerAuthPublicationTag.first_seal_guess` with its type ascription). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement, each old statement from the new theorem and each restated wrapper equal to its old body (253 proofs; with the seven twins, all 260 `#print axioms` lines are exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`), and its eleven doctored controls (among them a weaker bound, a dropped premise, an implicit binder made explicit, an equation turned into an inequation, a flipped Boolean in an event and three altered wrapper bodies) all differ from the base record; the ten that it ran through the equivalence proof are all rejected. Its declaration diff agrees with gate G4, the later-stage users of the seven twins agree with the stage's rename list, and its phase B probe reproduces the stage's exception list. The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, `git diff --check`, the set of changed files, the Lean diff counts, the residual and suppression scans, the twins, the phase B exceptions and the A3 sites, the hash-pinned and generated files, the inventory pins and digests, and the agreement of the documents and the receipt with the gate, certificate and inventory artifacts. Claude fixed all six findings on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S8` in the commit that adds this entry and the receipt `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s8-review.json`; none needs an owner decision.

Module docstring (EQ-S8-1, H1). The plan's codemod, which rewrote the deprecated probability notation of the stage's files, had also rewritten the notation quoted in prose. The module docstring of `PqxdhHypothesisWitness` said that `hb111_instance` repeats the HB-111 statement verbatim and that this statement uses the deprecated `Pr[= true | ...]` notation, so the deprecation linter is disabled for that declaration; after the codemod it called the forward notation `Pr{let x ← ...}[x = true]` deprecated, although the stage had removed the suppression it mentioned, and the restated `hb111_instance` parenthesizes the computation that HB-111 writes without parentheses, so the two statements are the same term but no longer the same text. No type, value, axiom guard, certificate or warning was affected. The sentence now says that `hb111_instance` repeats the HB-111 statement instantiated at the toy instance and, like HB-111, writes the KEM correctness-error term in the forward event notation; it names no linter option, so the inventory pin that the module has no deprecation suppression still holds. The codemod now rewrites code text only: comments, docstrings and string and character literals are cut out with the scanner of the step 7 source scan before the rewrite and put back unchanged afterwards, and each of them that the earlier tool would have changed is listed for review instead. Replayed on the stage's 76 input files, the corrected codemod writes the same files as the earlier one except for that docstring line, which it keeps and lists, and its report is otherwise the same as the stage's; in a synthetic file with the deprecated notation in code, in two docstrings, a line comment, a nested block comment, a string literal and a comment inside a probability expression, it rewrites the code exactly as before and leaves the six others unchanged. On the 132 files of stage S9 in the plan it finds no comment, docstring or literal that the earlier tool would have changed. A new check among the stage tools, run after each build, lists every changed line of the Lean sources that lies in a comment, docstring or literal, every wrapped `--` comment that a change adds and every comment that cites the migration's scratch notes; on the files of `f752e169` it reports this docstring line, the eight wrapped comments and the three references below, and on this tree only the reworded docstring line.

Proof comments (EQ-S8-2, H2). Eight `--` comments that the stage added to its proofs broke a sentence across two lines, against the repository's rule that text files have no mid-sentence line breaks (in `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCanonical`, `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx`, `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordIntegrity`, `PqxdhFirstRecordFinishBound`, three in `PqxdhFirstRecordStopTagged` and in `PqxdhServerAuthCollision`), and three of them cited numbered editing rules ("S6 editing rule 10", "S8 editing rule 14") that exist only in the migration's machine-local scratch notes. Each is now one line that states its content: the two alias proofs say that the law is stated over an abstract draw and applied after generalizing the uniform HMAC block, because the concrete draw exceeds the elaborator's recursion depth; the integrity proof gives the event form of `RawCtxSelectedFamilyNonceCoupling.marked_agree`; the four relational proofs name the relational exception of phase B; and `PqxdhServerAuthCollision.verified_third_probability` has one sentence before each of its two event-level laws, the impossible event of the rejection branches and the reassociation that a bare `rw [bind_assoc]` cannot do because it would also rewrite the bind that expands `Pr{…}`. No statement, proof step or declaration changed. The seven files are seven lines shorter in all; Lean rebuilt the seven modules and every module that imports one of the six whose compiled file changed, while the moved comment of `PqxdhServerAuthCollision` keeps every position in that file, so the modules that import it and no other changed module were reused.

Simplification bridges (H3). The S8 entry above and the analysis named the twelve declarations whose full simplifications the facade probe of the second full build found reaching the pin's simplification bridge, with their seventeen `simp` calls. The hand-rewritten proof of `PqxdhFirstBeaconRecordCtx.alias_bound` also excludes the three bridges in one call, so thirteen declarations of the stage, with eighteen calls, carry the exclusion, none of them before the stage; the S8 entry, the analysis and the S8 receipt now say so. Section 3 of the plan recommends exactly this exclusion; no proof changed.

Leases of checklist step 7 (H4). The S8 entry above gave the leases 00001526 to 00001531 for checklist step 7; that range also holds the facade, usage and used probes of gates G3 to G5 and the inventory check. Step 7 consists of the source scan and the exact axiom walk: the walk ran under lease 00001531 and takes as roots all declarations of the project's modules, as many as the final declaration dump of lease 00001526 lists, and the source scan and the twin-name scan run without a lease. The step 7 bullet of the S8 entry now says this.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S8/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below count the exclusions of the pin's simplification bridges exactly (eight `simp` calls in eight files exclude all three, and the full `simp` calls of `chance_split` and `hitAt_bound` exclude `evalDist_apply_singleton`; the sentence on what else was edited below now says so) and tell the 154 modules of the work set from the 113 that the stage edited (the first validation bullet and the gate G4 bullet below). At the owner's decision of 2026-10-19 (item 4: plan [`vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md`](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) with D1 to D5, D8 to D11 and phase B of D6 with a final sweep; item 5: Lean-only validation; item 6, of 2026-10-20: item 4 of amendment A2 accepted) and under amendments A1, A1.1, A2 and A3 of the plan, Claude implemented stage S9 (the `PqxdhRecord*`, `PqxdhRegistration*`, `PqxdhRoot*`, `PqxdhOpen*`, `PqxdhLater*`, `PqxdhDeployment*` and `PqxdhReceive*` families of section 7 of the plan) and the final sweep on branch `codex/vcvio-migration-S9` in a fresh clone of origin based on central `86bbc791bb3f2f8ad3747394fc68620101faa8ae` (stage S8, integrated on 2026-10-21), with the Lake packages copied from the S8 clone and the two-file proof flake equal to central's. The stage has 136 modules: the plan's 132 and four users of earlier transitional twins without deprecated uses of their own (`PqxdhDeploymentIdealKeyFirst`, which has no stage in the plan, `PqxdhKeyScheduleFromRoot`, which the S6 review fix EQ-S6-2 left to the final sweep, and `PqxdhLaterExecutableCompletion` and `PqxdhLaterSharedMessagePrefix`). Transitional twins and consumers. S9 is the last stage and adds no twin: the reference probe of the stage (60 edges) finds no user of a restated theorem outside the stage. The stage's files used all 23 twins left by earlier stages, with 86 references in 47 files: of stage S2 `PqxdhSecretInputKdf.evalSPMF_sample_comm`, `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good` and `UniformTargetSelection.chance_eq`; of stage S3 `PqxdhHiddenRoot.tvDist_bind_left_le_probEvent_bind`; of stage S4 `PqxdhDynamicIdentityPool.sampleSeeds_unused`, `PqxdhDynamicKemJointSource.selected_source`, `PqxdhDynamicKemJointStopping.history_event` and `pre_event`, `PqxdhJointIdentityFamily.sampleFamily_target_first`, `PqxdhJointPhase1Reports.fieldOrigin_advantage_le_sum` and `PqxdhKemCorrectnessError.error_probability`; of stage S6 `RawCtxFirstParentRisk.full_inputs`, `RawCtxPrefixKeyOrder.complete` and `source_game`, and `RawCtxSelectedInputCandidates.uniform_candidates`; of stage S7 `PqxdhInitialNativeRisk.carried_risk`; of stage S8 `PqxdhFirstRecordConfidentiality.post_source_augmented`, `pre_event` and `true_mass_eq`, `PqxdhHeadlineBridges.authenticated_extracted_root_bound` and `authenticated_native_root_bound_bridged_full`, `PqxdhServerAuthCtxMaterial.key_first` and `PqxdhServerAuthEntryFactor.eager_run`. `twin_rename.py` switched the 86 references back to the restated theorems (afterwards no file of the tree names a twin), and `twins.py remove` deleted the 23 twins in their 18 modules with their eight generated helpers. Their 23 per-twin pins in `check-inventory.sh` were retired and the 48 count pins of the 18 modules (plain and unused-variable twin headers, unused-variable switches and deprecation suppressions) were set to zero with a removal note. The stage's one further user of the adapter-stated `StatefulGoodEvent.event_good`, `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginMuteAdaptive.event_good`, uses the S6 option (b) route through the bridge in both directions (amendment A2 item 3). Amendment A3 item 3: the S4 bridge site `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthInjective.lower_le_advantage` is rewritten in the forward vocabulary against the restated `PqxdhJointIdentity.advantage` body; the forward-lemma sites of `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthInjectiveGame` and `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineInjective` stay forward.

What else was edited. The `𝒮[A] = 𝒮[B]` equations were converted from their exact bracket delimiters before the plan's codemod, which then rewrote the probability notation of 76 files, and all 52 suppression lines were deleted. The proofs of the stage's files were rewritten off the deprecated layer (phase B) with the forward laws of the pin and S0's backports, by scripted exact-text, count-checked edits and by hand, compiled layer by layer into an overlay of the base build before the full build. The codemod renamed the support-restricted monotonicity and bind laws to forward lemmas without the support hypothesis, so those calls use the `_of_support` forms; where a rewrite with `bind_assoc` or `pure_bind` would also rewrite the expansion of `Pr{…}`, the proofs reassociate the computation only or rewrite in the measure form first. The relational-class proofs reach VCVio's coupling laws over the deprecated distributions through the allowlisted bridge (`VCVioLegacy.evalSPMF_eq_iff_evalDistEq`), and the expected-value proofs reach the pin's expectation laws through `VCVioLegacy.probEvent_eq_prEvent`. Before the stage the stage's files used the pin's simplification bridges from point masses and events to the deprecated probabilities (`evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ`) on 172 lines in 67 files; the rewritten proofs use the forward point-mass and event laws instead (among them `prEvent_eq_evalDist_singleton`, `OracleComp.prEvent_eq_zero_iff` and `OracleComp.mem_support_iff_evalDist_singleton_pos`), and eight `simp` calls in eight files exclude the three bridges, as section 3 of the plan recommends. The facade probe of the overlay before the first full build found three declarations whose full `simp` calls still reached the bridge without naming it: `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineLor.chance_split` and `PqxdhRecordFactsLaterFresh.hitAt_bound`, whose full `simp` calls now exclude `evalDist_apply_singleton`, and `PqxdhRegistrationJointFinalReportEvent.transport`, which now uses `simp only` with forward lemmas. The four KEM steps of `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemGame`, `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemCertified`, `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemRawGame` and `PqxdhDeploymentLaterServerKemHop` close with the forward point-mass law and no longer list `SPMF.boolDistAdvantage` among their unfolding lemmas, which had made Lean generate its equation lemma, whose type uses the deprecated conversion to a measure, in `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemGame`. `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginOrdinalProbability` used the forbidden `OracleComp.ae_of_forall_mem_support` twice and now uses `OracleComp.ae_evalDist_of_forall_mem_support`. No statement outside the 54, no definition, import, axiom guard, option or comment changed.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S9/logs/`). All passed.

## VCVio deprecation migration, final sweep and acceptance check (2026-10-21)

Corrected in part: [the review fixes](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below add the Boolean advantage to the residual notions that LA-09 and the toolchain summary of the trust boundary name (the summary now also names the relational coupling laws), put the sentences of the trust boundary and the analysis that still described the generic discrete laws as stated with `evalSPMF` into the past, and restrict the Lean README's sentence to the statements that used the deprecated interface; the documentation paragraph below describes the documents as `617fac92` left them. This entry remains the record of `0844d00d` and `617fac92`.

Corrected again: [the confirming-review fixes of stage S9](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-confirming-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below make precise that the Boolean advantage stays on the pin's subprobability layer only through its bound by total variation: the pin defines the Boolean advantage itself from the measure-valued interface, and upstream removes it. They also rewrite 56 proofs, in modules of stages S2 and S4 to S9, whose steps rewrote the pin's expansion of `Pr{…}`, which the final acceptance check does not see.

After stage S9 (`6794f734`), on the same branch and clone, Claude carried out the final sweep of section 7 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) and its final acceptance check (section 9, with item 4 of amendment A2, which the owner accepted on 2026-10-20).

Whole-project phase B residual. The facade probe of the stage's build, run over every module of the project, found 325 declarations with deprecated or deprecated-stated VCVio constants outside the bridge. Each one in a module of a stage is in that stage's exception list (S1: the review fix's phase B report; S2: the non-twin and adapter declarations of its split; S3; S4; S5 with the reasons corrected in the S6 review fix; S6; S7; S8; S9). Of the 58 in modules of no stage, 40 are in the Lean model and refinement modules (`BeaconcryptCore.Model.*`, `BeaconcryptCore.Refinement.*`) and use only Lean's own deprecated `if_pos`, `if_neg`, `if_true` and `if_false`, outside the migration, and 18 use VCVio's deprecated layer.

What was edited (commit `0844d00d`, proofs only, in eight modules of no stage). `PqxdhDeploymentIdealFrame.lawEq_support_subset` used the forbidden `mem_support_iff_evalDist_singleton_ne_zero` and now uses `OracleComp.mem_support_iff_evalDist_singleton_pos`. `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginSelectedGuess.failed_trace_bound` used the forbidden `OracleComp.ae_of_forall_mem_support` and now uses `OracleComp.ae_evalDist_of_forall_mem_support`; it was found by the used-constant probe of the first sweep build, because its constant is not deprecated-stated and so is not in the facade probe. `PqxdhInitialLegalObserver.first_miss_observer` used `evalSPMF_bind_const_of_no_failure` and now uses `OracleComp.EvalDistEq.bind_const`; `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginGuess.independent_guess` and `PqxdhServerAuthJointIdentityCompiler.seed_order` used `DeferredSampling.evalSPMF_bind_comm` and now use `OracleComp.EvalDistEq.bind_bind_swap`. `PqxdhLaterChallengeGeneratedBeforeObservation.observed`, `PqxdhLaterChallengePrefixMass.prepared_before` and `PqxdhServerAuthObservedNative.native_game_source` converted the distribution equation of VCVio's relational coupling laws (`evalSPMF_map_eq_of_relTriple`, `evalSPMF_eq_of_relTriple_eqRel`) with `evalDist_eq_of_evalSPMF_eq`, which uses the pin's link between the discrete and the general semantics; they now convert through the allowlisted bridge `VCVioLegacy.evalSPMF_eq_iff_evalDistEq` and `EvalDistEq.evalDist_eq` (relational class). The 14 remaining declarations of such modules form the final sweep's exception list, classified by the S9 tool: nine of the expected-value class in `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineDctHash` (`beaconStep_coll`, `coll_world`, `draw_step`, `expected_countCoin`, `primitiveRun_step`, `primitive_step`, `quiet_step`, `serverStep_coll`, `world_step_coll`), two of the total-variation class (`PqxdhInitialSourceExperiment.normalization`, `PqxdhSelectedPostCompiler.source_distance`) and the three relational proofs above.

Documentation (this commit). LA-09 and the toolchain summary of [the trust boundary](../../beaconcrypt-core/proofs/trusted-boundary.md) now describe the forward interface and the remaining dependency on the pin's subprobability layer, as documentation without a change of trust; the Lean README's VCVio paragraph and the toolchain paragraph of [`doc/formal-verification.md`](../formal-verification.md) say that the migration is complete; and [the residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md) lists what remains for the upgrade (plan section 14).

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S9 and final sweep, review fixes (2026-10-21)

Corrected in part: [the confirming-review fixes of stage S9](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s9-and-final-sweep-confirming-review-fixes-2026-10-21) below found that the step of EQ-S9-1 was not the only proof step that relied on how the pin expands `Pr{…}` (19 proofs of stage S9 and 37 of earlier stages did), and make the Boolean-advantage wording of H4 precise. This entry remains the record of `9199ab6a`.

Two independent reviews of stage S9 and the final sweep at `617fac92f58ecb19a634ecbf41daef47d3420606` (step 6 of section 8 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md)), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19: a statement-equivalence review (verdict: approve with nits; finding EQ-S9-1) and a hygiene review (verdict: request changes; findings H1 to H4). Neither raised an owner item. The equivalence review confirmed the 54 certificates independently of the certificate tool. It made fresh whole-project probes of the base and stage builds (95,184 and 95,128 declarations; the 23 twins present at the base and absent at the stage), copied the old statement of each restated theorem verbatim from the source text of `86bbc791` into the stage environment and matched each copy with the fresh base record by `Expr.hash`, the `pp.all` text, the scope-erased fingerprint, the binder list and the universe levels (54 of 54, including `typed_root` with its binder annotation and the textual-fallback theorems `frozen_single` and `native_frozen`). In a compiled file it proved the iff of each new and old statement and each old statement from the restated theorem (108 proofs, every `#print axioms` line exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`), and its eleven doctored controls all differ from the base records. Its own declaration diff found type changes only in the 54 restated theorems and two generated `_simp_1_1` helpers of restated theorems, no definition-body change, proof changes only in the work set and the eight sweep theorems, all 69 uses of the 23 removed twins switched to the originals, every guard unchanged, and the phase B lists reproduced row for row (93 and 14 declarations, all within item 4 of amendment A2). The hygiene review checked the branch and its history, the commit footers, the scope, the pinned and generated files, the inventory, the receipt, the residual report and the documents.

Proof step of the A3 site (EQ-S9-1). The stage's rewrite of `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthInjective.lower_le_advantage` (amendment A3 item 3) proved its local equation `decided`, which equates the probability of the event `LowerCollision setup.joint target` on `sampleFamily (count + 1)` with the probability that `sampleFamily (count + 1) >>= fun seeds => pure (decide (LowerCollision setup.joint target seeds))` returns `true`, by `simp only [bind_assoc, pure_bind, decide_eq_true_eq]`. That closes only because the pin's macro expands `Pr{let x ← mx}[p x]` to `𝒟[do let x ← mx; return p x] {True}`, so the bind laws reach into the expansion, against section 3 of the plan and the stage's own rule. Upstream `afa6593f` elaborates the notation to `prEvent ((fun x => p x) <$> mx)`, on which these laws do not fire; since the step names no deprecated or removed constant, neither gate G5 nor the residual report counted it. The step now rewrites the computation only: a local equation from `bind_pure_comp` turns the bind into a map, `prEvent_map` moves the map into the event, and `prEvent_congr` with `decide_eq_true_iff` closes it; both event laws exist upstream with the same statements and explicit arguments. A replay-style check of the step over an arbitrary `ProbComp` computation and predicate, with `classical` as in the proof and with a decidable predicate (scoped leases 00002098, 00002100, 00002101 and 00002102), compiles against the pin and against the upstream `afa6593f` build of the forward replay, and the earlier step compiles against the pin and fails upstream with "`simp` made no progress". The statement of `lower_le_advantage`, certified in stage S9, its axiom guard and the rest of its proof are unchanged; the new step uses no deprecated constant and no bridge lemma. A one-line comment states why the computation is rewritten.

Module docstrings (H1). Four module docstrings of stage files described restated statements or replaced proof steps in the deprecated layer, which the stage had not updated. `PqxdhRecordLiftKeyCut` said that `key_cut` restated its law in the deprecated `𝒮`, that `miss_eager`, `table_eager`, `key_cut_view` and `key_cut` were stated in `𝒮` because `PqxdhServerAuthEntryFactor.eager_run` and `PqxdhRecordLiftProjectionCut.split_continuation` were, and that only these four switched off the deprecation linter, although all of them are restated `EvalDistEq` statements and the module has no suppression line; `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemSecondEntryTable` said that `indexed_eager` was stated in VCVio's deprecated `𝒮[…]` notation; `PqxdhDeploymentServerAuthEager` named `DeferredSampling.evalSPMF_bind_comm`, which its proofs replaced by `OracleComp.EvalDistEq.bind_bind_swap`; and `PqxdhRecordLiftLaterCtx` named `evalDist_eq_of_evalSPMF_eq`, which `key_cut_of` replaced by `EvalDistEq.evalDist_eq`. The four sentences now say that these statements are equalities in distribution (`EvalDistEq`), like the lemmas they apply, and name the laws the proofs use; each stays on its line, and none names a linter option, so the inventory pins that the modules have no deprecation suppression still hold. The reviewer's scan for backticked names in comments that the code no longer uses and for deprecated notation in comments, run on the edited tree, no longer reports the four lines and reports nothing new.

Documents (H2, H4). The trust boundary's paragraph on the completed compatibility repairs and the overview of the Lean 4.34 port in [the analysis](../formal-verification-analysis.md) said in the present tense that generic discrete laws use `evalSPMF`, which contradicted the updated toolchain summary: they now say that these laws were stated with `evalSPMF` until the VCVio deprecation migration restated them as `EvalDistEq` equalities. LA-09 now lists the Boolean advantage with total variation, expectations and the subdistribution adapter semantics among the notions that the pin defines on its subdistribution layer and that the recorded residual statements use; the toolchain summary lists the Boolean advantage and the relational coupling laws too, as the trust boundary's final-sweep section, the analysis and [the residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md) do. The Lean README's VCVio paragraph said that every statement outside the bridge is written with `Pr{…}[…]` and `EvalDistEq`; it now says this of every statement outside the bridge that used the deprecated interface, since many statements use the measure notation `𝒟[…]`, total variation or the Boolean advantage. Documentation only, no change of trust.

Counts (H3). The S9 entry and the analysis said that ten `simp` calls in ten files exclude the three simplification bridges and then named two of them separately. The stage adds exactly ten exclusion lines in ten files: eight `simp` and `simpa` calls exclude `evalDist_apply_singleton`, `evalDist_apply_setOf` and `evalDist_apply_univ`, and the full `simp` calls of `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineLor.chance_split` and `PqxdhRecordFactsLaterFresh.hitAt_bound` exclude `evalDist_apply_singleton` only. The S9 entry also called the 154 modules of the work set (the 136 stage modules and the 18 modules of the deleted twins) edited, and the analysis said that the edited files keep their 24 heartbeat and recursion options: the stage commit edits 113 of them, the 200 theorems with changed proofs are all in these 113, and the 113 files have 22 such options before and after, the 154 files 24 and the 136 stage files 15. The S9 entry and the analysis now say so; the substantive claim, that no option was added, holds. The stage receipt `vcvio-deprecations-s9.json` ("24 in the stage's files before and after") is left as the record of `617fac92`, and the review-fix receipt records the correction.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S9/review-fix/logs/`). All passed.

- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged) before `coord.sh inv rehash` of the seven changed rows (the five Lean files, `trusted-boundary.md` and the Lean README; `check-inventory.sh` and `BeaconcryptCore.lean` unchanged, no pin changed), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00002111): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".

## VCVio deprecation migration, stage S9 and final sweep, confirming-review fixes (2026-10-21)

A confirming review of the review fixes at `9199ab6a55f0f2ae3793bf59178978619857f2c2`, Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19, found the findings of both reviews resolved and raised two new ones: a minor finding on proof steps (C1-1) and a nit on the wording of the Boolean advantage (C1-2); verdict: request changes. Neither needs an owner decision, and no statement, assumption, contract or criterion changes.

Proof steps through the pin's expansion of the event notation (C1-1). At the pin, `Pr{let x ← mx}[p x]` is a macro for `𝒟[mx >>= fun x => pure (p x)] {True}`; upstream VCVio `afa6593f` elaborates the same text to `prEvent ((fun x => p x) <$> mx)`. The fix of EQ-S9-1 replaced one proof step that applied bind laws to the bind that the macro adds. The confirming review found twelve more such steps in modules of stage S9 (`conv_lhs => rw [bind_assoc, OracleComp.prEvent_bind_const]` and its right-hand twin in `PqxdhDeploymentKemTwoSided`, `PqxdhDeploymentLaterKemTwoSided`, `PqxdhDeploymentLaterServerBridge` and `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemRawBridge`; `rw [bind_assoc, OracleComp.prEvent_bind_const]` in `PqxdhRegistrationJointKemRawGame` and `PqxdhDeploymentLaterServerKemHop`; `simp only [bind_assoc, pure_bind, …]` in `PqxdhLaterInputAlias`, `PqxdhRecordLiftProjectionCut`, `PqxdhRecordLiftSeedCut`, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineAnomaly`, `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineCollision` and `PqxdhLaterCommonRisk`) and named `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginFailedCallback` and `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginFailedFlag` as candidates. Over abstract computations each of these steps compiles against the pin and fails against the upstream build. Such a step names no removed constant, so neither gate G5 nor the residual report sees it. To find all of them rather than those a text search shows, the fixes add a probe of the elaborated proof terms: in every declaration outside the shim and bridge modules it reports each application of a theorem that equates two computations of a generic result type, instantiated at `Prop` (a monad or functor law applied to the `Prop`-valued computation of the macro), and each use of a theorem stated for `Prop`-valued computations or continuations. On the build of `9199ab6a` it reports 56 declarations: 19 in modules of stage S9 (the twelve steps, three proofs in the two candidate modules, and `PqxdhDeploymentHeadlineLor.chance_split`, `PqxdhDeploymentProvenance.client_transfer`, `PqxdhRegistrationJointFinalReportEvent.transport` and `PqxdhRegistrationR1RootProbability.prefix_probability`, whose full or `simpa` simplifications reached the macro) and 37 in modules of earlier stages (S2 1, S4 3, S5 11, S6 7, S7 11, S8 4). On the build of the stage's base `86bbc791` it reports exactly these 37, so stage S9 added the 19. The laws involved are `bind_assoc`, `pure_bind`, `bind_pure_comp`, `map_bind`, `map_pure` and `Functor.map_map` at `Prop` and the flattened laws `OracleComp.prEvent_bind_const`, `OracleComp.prEvent_bind_mono_of_support` and `OracleComp.prEvent_bind_congr_of_support` (stage S4 had recommended the flattened forms). All 56 proofs were rewritten, by 76 scripted exact-text edits in 49 files, as section 3 of the plan asks: an unused draw is dropped with `OracleComp.EvalDistEq.bind_const` and its event form; a final `pure` becomes a map of the computation with `bind_pure_comp` given the draw or the result type, and `prEvent_map` moves the map into the event; bind laws are restricted to the computation's result type; comparisons after a common draw use `prEvent_bind_mono_of_forall_le_of_support` and `prEvent_bind_congr` instead of the flattened laws; computations are normalized as Boolean point masses (`prEvent_eq_evalDist_singleton`, `prEvent_eq_evalDist_decide`) and converted back; pure and impossible branches close with `prEvent_pure`, `prEvent_false`, `prEvent_eq_zero_of_forall_not` and `OracleComp.prEvent_eq_zero_iff`; full simplifications run only on goals without an event, after a point mass is abstracted where needed. `PqxdhServerAuthCollision.verified_third_probability`, whose rejection branches collapsed the macro into `𝒟[(pure False : ProbComp Prop)] {True}`, now decides its event into a Boolean point mass first, and its local lemma states `𝒟[(pure false : ProbComp Bool)] {true} = 0`. The probe of the rebuilt tree reports none of these declarations. Every replacement step shape, over abstract computations, compiles without a diagnostic against the pin and against the upstream build `afa6593f` (30 examples), and the five replaced shapes of the earlier stages' modules compile against the pin and fail upstream. What the probe cannot see is left to the upgrade build, as [the residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md) now says: a rewrite with a map law that upstream's final map of the event would also match, and a full simplification whose upstream simp set differs. Four rewritten proofs of stage S9 and one of stage S7 no longer need an exclusion of the pin's simplification bridges: in stage S9 five `simp` and `simpa` calls now exclude all three bridges and one only the point-mass bridge, and in stage S7 eight declarations with 14 calls carry the exclusion.

Boolean advantage (C1-2). The review fixes above added the Boolean advantage to the notions that LA-09 and the toolchain summary of the trust boundary place on the pin's subprobability layer. The pin defines `ProbComp.boolDistAdvantage` from the measure-valued `𝒟`, states its law against point masses in measure form, and no project declaration uses `SPMF.boolDistAdvantage`; only the bound of the Boolean advantage by total variation belongs to that layer, and the Boolean advantage is upgrade residual because upstream removes it. LA-09, the toolchain summary and the final-sweep section of the trust boundary, [the residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md) and the analysis's paragraph on the final acceptance check now say so.

Documents. Notes at the top of the entries of stages S2, S4, S7, S8 and S9, of the final sweep and of the first review fixes point here where this round contradicts or changes their sentences; [the analysis](../formal-verification-analysis.md) corrects the same sentences in its stage paragraphs and adds a paragraph on this review; the residual report adds a section on proof steps that depend on the pin's event notation. The receipt is `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-s9-review2.json`.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/S9/review-fix-2/logs/`). All passed.

- Before the full build, scoped single-file checks of the 49 edited modules against the stage build had no error and no warning, and the probe over a copy of the build with these modules recompiled reported no declaration.
- `make check-lean` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to central's), on the edited tree (lease 00002372, every Lean job in a memory-capped scope with six Lean workers): 5,535 jobs, 1,827 modules rebuilt, rc 0, no error, so every axiom guard holds; the warning categories against the stage's base build pass as before (VCVio deprecation warnings 36 to none). The 2,909 warning and error records equal those of the check of `9199ab6a`, and the rebuilt modules are exactly the 49 edited ones, every module that imports one of them and the root.
- Post-build measurements (scoped leases). The declaration dump has 95,133 declarations: against that of `9199ab6a` only the proof terms of the 56 rewritten theorems change, five `simp` helpers that Lean generates for the new `simp only` calls with hypotheses are added (in `AdaptiveCtxPrefixValidation`, `CtxComputationalPrivacy`, `CtxNonceAeadIndDollarValidation`, `PqxdhDeploymentProvenance` and `PqxdhRecordFactsOriginFailedFlag`), and one generated helper of `PqxdhLaterInputAlias.prepared_collision` changes its binder-aware fingerprint only; no declaration is removed and no hand-written statement changes. The facade probe is byte-identical to that of `9199ab6a`. The statement-name probe is byte-identical to that of `9199ab6a`. The usage and used probes have the same constants as for `9199ab6a`; only the numbers of users change, as the rewritten proofs use the event laws (`prEvent_map`, `prEvent_bind_congr`, `prEvent_eq_evalDist_singleton`, `prEvent_eq_evalDist_decide`, `prEvent_eq_zero_of_forall_not`) and support laws more and the point-mass facts `MeasureTheory.Measure.dirac_apply_singleton_true`, `evalDist_pure` and `OracleComp.evalDist_map_const` less, which the rewriting of the expansion had used. The source scan finds the same 28 sites, all in the bridge, and the same 54 comment mentions. `LEAN-SCAN files 1994 hits 0`; `TWIN-NAMES-IN-COMMENTS files 1994 hits 0`; the prose check against `9199ab6a` prints `PROSE-CHECK files 49 modified 1 added 0 removed 0 wrapped-added 0 scratch-refs 0` (the comment on the local lemma of `PqxdhServerAuthCollision`); the exact axiom walk of checklist step 7 of [the completion handover](handover/completion-2026-10-11/README.md) (lease 00002385) reaches 121,982 constants from the 95,133 roots with none unresolved, prints `[Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]` and the same server-domain lines as the final sweep's. The probe of the build (lease 00002386) reports no declaration.
- The phase B report, the stage's and the sweep's exception lists (93 and 14 declarations) and the whole-project residual, rerun on these inputs, equal those of `9199ab6a`; the cumulative G4 comparison from the plan's base and the final acceptance check differ only by the five added generated helpers (83 instead of 78, 95,133 declarations), and the final acceptance check prints `RESULT PASS`.
- Certificates (leases 00002387 and 00002388), with byte-identical copies of the stage's certificate file and negative controls: 52 inverse-map certificates pass and the two textual fallbacks have the dump's types, each kernel-checked with exactly `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`; the nine negative controls fail at lines 13 to 21; the checker reports `RESULT PASS`.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged) before `coord.sh inv rehash` of 50 changed rows (the 49 Lean files and `trusted-boundary.md`; `check-inventory.sh` and `BeaconcryptCore.lean` unchanged, no pin changed), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (lease 00002389): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary".
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only Lean files and documents change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-21: both findings of the confirming review are fixed; stage S9 and the final sweep are ready for integration into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.

## VCVio deprecation migration, closing checks of phase B (stage PB) (2026-10-21)

Closing step of the [VCVio deprecation migration](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md) after the integration of stage S9 and the final sweep into central (`67e2cefc1a83058e87add9ac6fa679d0ade852a0`), Lean-only by the owner's decision item 5 of 2026-10-19. The owner's decision item 4 of 2026-10-19 included phase B (D6) with a final sweep after S9, and item 6 accepted item 4 of amendment A2: the target is zero uses of the deprecated layer in proofs outside `VCVioLegacyBridge` apart from recorded exceptions whose dependency or semantics exists at the pin only in the deprecated form. Section 7 of the plan puts the final sweep into stage S9, which [did it](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-final-sweep-and-acceptance-check-2026-10-21). This step therefore changes no Lean file: it confirms the end state on `67e2cefc`, reruns the certificates of all stages together against the final build, and corrects four passages of the migration's documents that the integration of S9 carried as open nits (C2-1, C2-2, CONF-S8-1, CF1-1). No statement, proof, assumption, contract, guard, import, inventory row or pin changes.

End state on `67e2cefc`:

- Transitional twins: no `_vcvioLegacy` name in any Lean source and no twin header; S9 removed the last 23.
- Deprecated syntax: the lexer scan of the plan finds 28 deprecated sites in the 1,994 Lean files, all in `VCVioLegacyBridge`, and no `set_option linter.deprecated false` outside it; the step 7 source scan of the completion checklist has 0 hits.
- Warnings: the build of `67e2cefc` (central integration replay, `lake --rehash build --no-build`, all 5,535 jobs up to date) has no VCVio deprecation warning in the project.
- Phase B: 321 declarations still reference deprecated or deprecated-stated VCVio constants outside the bridge. 40 of them use only Lean core's deprecated `if_pos`, `if_neg`, `if_true` and `if_false`, which are not VCVio's and are outside the migration. Each of the other 281 is in its stage's exception list (267) or in the final sweep's (14), with a class whose dependency or semantics exists at the pin only in the deprecated form: total variation 134, expectation 90, relational 29, adapter 14, total variation and relational 12, façade-stated dependency 2 ([residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md)). The uses outside the bridge of façade lemmas that do have a forward replacement at the pin (`probEvent_pure`, `probOutput_bind_eq_tsum`, `probEvent_eq_tsum_ite`, `probFailure_uniformSample` and the `SPMF` support lemmas) were checked one by one: each is inside a recorded declaration of the adapter, expectation or relational class, whose other dependencies exist at the pin only in the deprecated form.
- Final acceptance check (plan section 9 with item 4 of A2) of the `67e2cefc` build, from the post-build measurements of the S9 confirming-review fixes on the same Lean sources: `RESULT PASS`.
- Forward names: of the 171 replacement names of the survey of the pin, the final build uses 51 VCVio constants, and all 51 exist at upstream `afa6593f`. The 124 VCVio, ToMathlib and PolyFun constants that the project uses and upstream lacks are the old layer used by the bridge and by the recorded exceptions, and the non-deprecated API that upstream removed (plan section 10); both are listed in the residual report.

Cumulative certificates. The certificate file of this step concatenates the certificate files of stages S1 to S9 without their 66 transitional-twin checks; each stage keeps its own section, so its `open`, `variable` and option commands stay local, and each certificate keeps its stage's pre-stage dump. It runs against the final build (the published `LEAN_PATH` of `67e2cefc`) with a fresh compile of the out-of-build certificate tool, byte-identical to the shared one. 749 inverse-map certificates pass and the 7 textual fallbacks have the dump's types (S2 `honest_decapsulation`, S3 `evalSPMF_first32_uniformJointKdfStream`, S4 `projectedOutput_uniform`, S6 `random`, S7 `bare_uniform`, S9 `frozen_single` and `native_frozen`): 756 declarations, exactly the union of the stages' restated declarations (720 theorems and 36 definitions, the restated union of the cumulative G4 comparison). Each certificate is kernel-checked with axioms among `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, and the run has no error and no warning, so every axiom guard of the fallbacks holds. The 54 negative controls of the stages that name no twin (the 8 twin controls are dropped) each fail at their line. Every restated statement of the migration therefore keeps its certified meaning in the final build.

Documents (the nits carried by the S9 integration). C2-1: the final-acceptance paragraph of [the analysis](../formal-verification-analysis.md) gives both declaration counts of the final tree (95,128; 95,133 after the confirming-review fixes added five generated `simp` helpers). C2-2: the [residual report](vcvio-upgrade-residual.md) section on proof steps that depend on the pin's event notation adds that the expansion probe does not see laws about measures or about values in ℝ≥0∞ applied at `Prop`, and names the one such step, the bridge `simpa` of `PqxdhInitialSourceLaterMixture.supported_event_bound`, a recorded total-variation exception; the report's upgrade steps also state the forward-name result. CONF-S8-1: a note at the top of [the S8 review-fix entry](#vcvio-deprecation-migration-stage-s8-review-fixes-2026-10-21) says what the moved comment of `PqxdhServerAuthCollision` kept. CF1-1: amendment A4 of the [plan](vcvio-deprecation-migration-plan.md#a4-claude-2026-10-21-stage-pb-inventory-pins-of-retired-transitional-twins) records that the count pins of retired twins stay at zero (204 pins of 51 modules) instead of being removed. The analysis adds a section on these checks; the receipt is `proof-receipts/vcvio-deprecations-pb.json`.

Validation, Claude's runs (machine-local, transient logs in `/tmp/bc-arc-scratch/vcvio-migration/PB/logs/`). All passed.

- No Lean file changes: the commit changes four documents and adds the receipt. The full build of `67e2cefc` (central integration replay, all 5,535 jobs up to date; `make check-lean` of the committed S9 confirming-review fixes, lease 00002391) is therefore the build of this tree, and no `lake build` was run.
- Step 7 of the completion checklist: the source scan of the 1,994 Lean files has 0 hits; the exact axiom walk against the published `LEAN_PATH` of `67e2cefc` (lease 00002404) reaches 121,982 constants from 95,133 roots with 0 unresolved and the axioms `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, and its output equals that of the S9 confirming-review fixes.
- Certificates (leases 00002401 to 00002403): as above; the checker reports `RESULT PASS`.
- `coord.sh inv recount` (1,987, unchanged) before `coord.sh inv rehash` (no row changed: no inventoried file is edited), then `make check-inventory` in the proof shell (two-file flake copy equal to the branch's): "Formal-verification inventory matches the reviewed boundary" on `67e2cefc` (lease 00002405) and on the edited tree (lease 00002406).
- Not run, by the owner's decision item 5 (only documents change): `verify-in-shell`, ProVerif, the transcript-fidelity test, `cargo test`, the Go and Python tests and the mutation suite.

Status, 2026-10-21: the closing checks of phase B are complete; this documentation commit has not had the two independent reviews of section 8 of the plan and is not integrated into central. `nix-collect-garbage` was not run.
